# Rashi on Ezra

רש"י על עזרא

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:1)


**וּבִשְׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס.** סֵדֶר סֵפֶר זֶה סָדוּר וּמָשׁוּךְ אַחַר סֵפֶר דָּנִיֵּאל כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּבָבָא בַּתְרָא (בבא בתרא טו.), וְהַמִּלָּה מוּסֶבֶת מִזֶּה לָזֶה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּדָנִיֵּאל בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְמָלְכוֹ ״אֲנִי דָנִיֵּאל בִּינֹתִי בַּסְּפָרִים מִסְפַּר הַשָּׁנִים אֲשֶׁר הָיָה דְבַר ה׳ אֶל יִרְמְיָה הַנָּבִיא לְמַלֹּאות לְחָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה״ (דניאל ט:ב), וְכֵן הוֹלֶכֶת וּמְסַפֶּרֶת כָּל הַפָּרָשָׁה בְּחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת וּזְמַן גָּלוּת בָּבֶל וְאֶת וִדּוּיוֹ אֲשֶׁר הִתְוַדָּה עַל חַטַּאת יִשְׂרָאֵל. וּתְחִלַּת סֵדֶר סֵפֶר זֶה כָּךְ הוּא, שֶׁלְּאַחַר שֶׁנֶּהֱרַג בֵּלְשַׁצַּר מָלַךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְדָרְיָוֶשׁ מָדָאָה קַבֵּל מַלְכוּתָא וְגוֹמֵר״ (דניאל ו:א), וּלְאַחַר מִיתַת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ מָלַךְ כּוֹרֶשׁ זֶה מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס, **וּבִשְׁנַת אַחַת** לְמָלְכוֹ הֻשְׁלְמוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה לִפְקִידַת גָּלוּת בָּבֶל מִיּוֹם שֶׁגָּלָה יְהוֹיָקִים שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לְפִי מְלֹאת לְבָבֶל שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה אֶפְקֹד אֶתְכֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיהו כט:י), שֶׁחָזְרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִגָּלוּת בָּבֶל לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבְאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה **בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ** יָסְדוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל יְסוֹד בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְצָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין הִלְשִׁינוּ עֲלֵיהֶם לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס וְצִוָּה לְבַטֵּל הַמְּלָאכָה, שֶׁלֹּא לִבְנוֹת עוֹד בִּנְיַן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וְהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּטֵלִים שֶׁלֹּא בָנוּ הַבַּיִת כָּל מַלְכוּת כּוֹרֶשׁ וַאֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ שֶׁמָּלַךְ אַחֲרָיו עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְמַלְכוּת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ בֶּן אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס, שֶׁהוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ בֶּן אֶסְתֵּר, וּבִשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְמַלְכוּתוֹ הִתְחִילוּ לִבְנוֹת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ עַד אֲשֶׁר הֻשְׁלַם הַבִּנְיָן, וּמִשְּׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ בֶּן אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס שֶׁנִּבְנָה הַבַּיִת בְּיָמָיו הָיוּ שְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה, לְקַיֵּם מַה שֶּׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לְמַלֹּאות לְחָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה וְגוֹ׳״ (דניאל ט:ב), וְעַכְשָׁיו בִּשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הֻשְׁלְמוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה לְמַלֹּאות לְחָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם חֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת שֶׁגָּלָה צִדְקִיָּהוּ. וּמִגָּלוּת יְהוֹיָקִים עַד גָּלוּת צִדְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁנֶּחֱרַב הַבַּיִת הָיוּ שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה, וְעַכְשָׁיו **בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס** הֻשְׁלְמוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה מִגָּלוּת יְהוֹיָקִים שֶׁגָּלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבָבֶל, וּבְכָךְ נִתְקַיֵּם הַמִּקְרָא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כִּי לְפִי מְלֹאת לְבָבֶל שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה אֶפְקֹד אֶתְכֶם וְגוֹמֵר״ (ירמיהו כט:י), וְזוֹ הִיא הַפְּקִידָה שֶׁחָזְרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל אַדְמָתָם וּבָנוּ יְסוֹד בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמַר עַכְשָׁיו. נִמְצָא שֶׁהָיָה חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַיִם שָׁנָה מִגָּלוּת צִדְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁנֶּחֱרַב הַבַּיִת עַד שָׁנָה אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס, וּשְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לְאַחַר שָׁנָה אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ שֶׁהִיא שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס, הֻשְׁלְמוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה לְחָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם מִגָּלוּת צִדְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁנֶּחֱרַב הַבַּיִת עַד שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ שֶׁהִתְחִילוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לִבְנוֹת הַבִּנְיָן וּגְמָרוּהוּ. וְכֵן מוֹכִיחַ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (פרק כט) וּבִמְסֶכֶת מְגִלָּה (מגילה יא:) וּבְסֵפֶר זֶה כַּאֲשֶׁר בֵּאַרְתִּי:


**And in the first year of Cyrus, the king of Persia** This book is arranged as a continuation of the Book of Daniel, as is explained in Baba Bathra (15a), and the word [וּבְשְׁנַת] refers back from this [book] to that one, for it is stated in Daniel (9:2): “In the first year of his reign, I, Daniel, contemplated the calculations of the number of the years that the word of the Lord had come to Jeremiah the prophet from the destruction of Jerusalem, seventy years.” And so the entire chapter proceeds to relate [the story] of the destruction of the Temple, the time of the Babylonian exile, and his confession, in which he confessed Israel’s sin. The beginning of this book is as follows: After Belshazzar was assassinated, Darius the Mede reigned, as it says (Dan. 6:1): “And Darius the Mede received the kingdom etc.” and after Darius’ death, this Cyrus, the king of Persia, reigned, and in the first year of his reign, the seventy years for the remembrance of the Babylonian exile, [counting] from the day that Jehoiakim was exiled, were completed, as it says (Jer. 29:10): “At the completion of seventy years of Babylon, I will remember you etc.” When Israel returned from the Babylonian exile to the land of Israel, in that year, in the first year of Cyrus, Israel laid the foundation of the Temple, and the adversaries of Judah and Benjamin slandered them to Cyrus, the king of Persia, and he commanded [them] to curtail the work, no longer to build the edifice of the Temple; and the Israelites were idle, for they did not build the Temple during the entire reign of Cyrus and Ahasuerus, who succeeded him, until the second year of Darius, the son of Ahasuerus, the king of Persia, who was Darius, the son of Esther. And in the second year of his reign, they began building the Temple until the building was completed. And from the first year of Cyrus until the second year of Darius, the son of Ahasuerus the king of Persia, in whose days the Temple was built, were eighteen years, to fulfill what was said (Dan. 9:2): “from the destruction of Jerusalem, seventy years etc.” And now, in the second year of Darius, seventy years had elapsed since the destruction of Jerusalem; [i.e.,] the destruction of the Temple, when Zedekiah was exiled. From the exile of Jehoiakim until the exile of Zedekiah, when the Temple was destroyed, were eighteen years, and now in the first year of Cyrus, the king of Persia, seventy years since the exile of Jehoiakim, when Israel was exiled to Babylon, were completed, and thereby the verse that states (Jer. 29:10): “for at the completion of seventy years of Babylon I will remember you etc.” was fulfilled. And this is the remembrance, that Israel returned to their soil and built the foundation of the building of the Temple, although it was not yet completed. We find that there were fifty-two years from the exile of Zedekiah, when the Temple was destroyed, until the first year of Cyrus, the king of Persia; and eighteen years after the first year of Cyrus, which is the second year of Darius, when Israel commenced to build the edifice, and they completed it. This is proven in Seder Olam (ch. 29), in Tractate Megillah (11b) and in this book, as I explained.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:2)


**לִכְלוֹת דְּבַר ה׳ מִפִּי יִרְמְיָה.** לִזְמַן אֲשֶׁר הֻשְׁלַם דְּבָרוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר דִּבֵּר יִרְמְיָהוּ הַנָּבִיא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו כט:י): ״כִּי לְפִי מְלֹאת לְבָבֶל וְגוֹ׳״:


**at the completion of the word of the Lord from the mouth of Jeremiah** at the time that the word of the Holy One, blessed be He, which Jeremiah spoke, was completed, as it says (Jer. 29:10): “for at the completion of seventy years of Babylon etc.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:3)


**הֵעִיר ה׳ אֶת רוּחַ.** רָצוֹן:


**the Lord aroused the spirit** the will.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:4)


**קוֹל.** כָּרוֹז הִכְרִיז בְּכָל מַלְכוּתוֹ:


**a proclamation** He proclaimed a proclamation throughout his kingdom.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:5)


**וְגַם בְּמִכְתָּב.** וְאַף מִכְתָּבוֹ שָׁלַח בְּכָל מַלְכוּתוֹ עַל דָּבָר זֶה:


**and also in writing** And he also sent his letter throughout his kingdom about this matter.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:1:6)


**לֵאמֹר.** וְכֹה אָמַר לָהֶם בְּהַכְרֵז הַכָּרוֹז אֲשֶׁר הִכְרִיז וּבְאִגֶּרֶת מִכְתָּבוֹ אֲשֶׁר שָׁלַח בְּכָל מַלְכוּתוֹ:


**saying** And so he said to them in his proclamation that he proclaimed and in his epistle that he sent throughout his kingdom.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:2:1)


**כֹּה אָמַר כּוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס וְגוֹ׳ נָתַן לִי.** שֶׁאֲנִי מֶלֶךְ וְשַׁלִּיט עַל כָּל הַמַּמְלָכוֹת:


**So said Cyrus, the king of Persia...delivered to me** that I am the king and ruler over all the kingdoms.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:2:2)


**וְהוּא פָקַד עָלַי.** צִוָּה עָלַי עַל יְדֵי יְשַׁעְיָה הַנָּבִיא לִבְנוֹת לוֹ בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר יְשַׁעְיָה (ישעיה מד:כח): ״הָאוֹמֵר לְכוֹרֶשׁ רֹעִי וְכָל חֶפְצִי יַשְׁלִים וְלֵאמֹר לִירוּשָׁלַיִם תִּבָּנֶה וְהֵיכָל תִּוָּסֵד״ (ישעיה מה:א), ״כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ לִמְשִׁיחוֹ לְכוֹרֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר הֶחֱזַקְתִּי בִימִינוֹ וְגוֹמֵר״. וּכְבָר נֶאֶמְרָה נְבוּאָה זוֹ עַל יְדֵי יְשַׁעְיָה:


**and He commanded me** He commanded me through Isaiah the prophet to build for Him the Temple in Jerusalem, as it says in the Book of Isaiah (44:28): “Who says to Cyrus, ‘He is My shepherd, and all My desire he shall fulfill,’ and to say to Jerusalem, ‘It shall be built, and the Temple shall be founded.’” (ibid. 45:1): “So said the Lord to His anointed one, to Cyrus, whose right hand I held....” And this prophecy was already stated by lsaiah.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:2:3)


**בִּירוּשָׁלִַם אֲשֶׁר בִּיהוּדָה.** אֲשֶׁר בִּמְדִינַת יְהוּדָה:


**in Jerusalem, which is in Judea** whch is in the province of Judea.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:3:1)


**מִי בָכֶם.** כָּךְ הָיָה מַכְרִיז וְכוֹתֵב לְכָל הַמַּמְלָכוֹת: מִי בָכֶם מִכָּל עַמּוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, יְהִי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּעֶזְרוֹ, וְיַעֲלֶה לִבְנוֹת בֵּיתוֹ אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**Who is among you** So did he announce and write to all the kingdoms, “Who is among you of all His people,” [i.e., of the people] of the Holy One, blessed be He, may the Holy One, blessed be He, be at his assistance, and let him ascend to build His house that is in Jerusalem.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:4:1)


**וְכָל הַנִּשְׁאָר.** וְכָל יְהוּדִי הַנִּשְׁאָר בִּמְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לַעֲלוֹת מֵחֲמַת שֶׁאֵין לוֹ מָמוֹן:


**And whoever remains** and every Jew who remains in his place, who cannot ascend because he has no money.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:4:2)


**יְנַשְּׂאוּהוּ.** מְצַוֶּה אֲנִי לְאַנְשֵׁי מְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁיַּעֲנִיקוּהוּ וִינַשְּׂאוּהוּ בְּכֶסֶף וּבְזָהָב וּרְכוּשׁ וּבְהֵמוֹת לְמַעַן יוּכַל לַעֲלוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם עִם הַנְּדָבָה אֲשֶׁר יִתְנַדְּבוּ אַנְשֵׁי מְקוֹמוֹ לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת, שֶׁאַף הָאֻמּוֹת יֵשׁ בָּהֶם אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ מִתְנַדְּבִים נְדָבָה לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת:


**shall help him** I command the people of his place to provide him and help him with silver, gold, possessions and cattle, in order that he be able to ascend to Jerusalem with the donation that the people of his place will donate for the building of the Temple, for also among the nations there were some who contributed donations to the building of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:4:3)


**לְבֵית הָאֱלֹהִים אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** מִן ״כֹּה אָמַר כּוֹרֶשׁ״ עַד כָּאן הַכְרָזַת הַכָּרוֹז וּשְׁלִיחוּת אִגֶּרֶת הַמִּכְתָּב:


**to the House of God, which is in Jerusalem** From “So said Cyrus” until here is the proclamation and the message of the written epistle.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:5:1)


**לִיהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין.** אוֹתָן רָאשֵׁי הָאָבוֹת אֲשֶׁר לַשְּׁבָטִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין:


**of Judah and Benjamin** the heads of the families of the tribes of Judah and Benjamin.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:5:2)


**לְכָל הָעִיר.** עִם כָּל אוֹתָם שֶׁהֵעִיר ה׳ רוּחוֹ לַעֲלוֹת וְלִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת, עָלוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם לִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת:


**all whom... inspired** They ascended to Jerusalem to build the Temple with all those whom God inspired to ascend and to build the Temple.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:6:1)


**וְכָל סְבִיבוֹתֵיהֶם.** וְכָל הָעַמִּים שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁכֵנֵיהֶם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל סִיְּעוּ וְהֶחֱזִיקוּ יְדֵי הַיְּהוּדִים **בְּזָהָב וּבְכֶסֶף** וּמַתָּנוֹת הַלָּלוּ לְמַעַן יוּכְלוּ לַעֲלוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**And all those around them** And all the nations who were Israel’s neighbors assisted and strengthened the hands of the Jews with gold and silver and all these gifts in order that they be able to ascend to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:6:2)


**לְבַד עַל כָּל הִתְנַדֵּב.** כָּל זֹאת הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים שְׁכֵינֵי הַיְּהוּדִים לְבַד מַה שֶּׁהֵם מִתְנַדְּבִים בְּעַצְמָם לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת:


**besides all that was donated** All this the neighbors of the Jews did, besides what they [the Jews] themselves donated for the building of the Temple.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:7:1)


**וַיִּתְּנֵם בְּבֵית אֱלֹהָיו.** אֲשֶׁר נְתָנָם נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּבֵית עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁלּוֹ:


**and had placed them in the temple of his god** that Nebuchadnezzar had placed in the temple of his pagan deity.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:8:1)


**עַל יַד מִתְרְדָת הַגִּזְבָּר.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל גִּזְבָּר אֲשֶׁר לְכוֹרֶשׁ, וְצִוָּהוּ כּוֹרֶשׁ לְהוֹצִיאָם:


**by the hand of Mithderath the treasurer** such was the name of Cyrus’s treasurer, and Cyrus commanded [him] to take them out.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:8:2)


**וַיִּסְפְּרֵם.** הַגִּזְבָּר הַזֶּה מָנָה אוֹתָם וּמְסָרָם לְשֵׁשְׁבַּצַּר לְמַעַן יִשָּׂאֵם וְיוֹלִיכֵם לִירוּשָׁלַיִם. וְאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ: הוּא שֵׁשְׁבַּצַּר הוּא דָנִיֵּאל, וְלָמָּה נִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ שֵׁשְׁבַּצַּר? שֶׁעָמַד בְּשֵׁשׁ צָרוֹת:


**and he counted them out** This treasurer counted them and delivered them to Sheshbazzar, so that he would carry them and transport them to Jerusalem. Our Rabbis said: “Sheshbazzar is identical with Daniel, and why was he called Sheshbazzar? Because he endured six troubles (שֵּׁשּׁ צָרוֹת) .”


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:8:3)


**הַנָּשִׂיא לִיהוּדָה.** הוּא הַנָּשִׂיא אֲשֶׁר לְשֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה:


**the prince of Judah** He is the prince of the tribe of Judah.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:9:1)


**מִסְפָּרָם** שֶׁל כְּלֵי בֵּית ה׳:


**their number** of the vessels of the House of the Lord.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:9:2)


**אֲגַרְטְלֵי.** מִינֵי כֵלִים הֵם:


**basins** Heb. אֲגַרְטְלֵי. They are kinds of vessels.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:9:3)


**מַחֲלָפִים.** הֵם סַכִּינִים, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן בֵּית הַחֲלָפוֹת, שֶׁעַל כֵּן שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹתְנִין בָּהּ הַסַּכִּינִין הָיְתָה נִקְרֵאת לִשְׁכַּת בֵּית הַחֲלָפוֹת:


**knives** Heb. מַחֲלָפִים. They are knives, and this expression refers to the chamber of the knives (בֵּית הַחֲלִיפוֹת). Because they would deposit the knives therein, it was called the chamber of the depository of the knives (לִשְּׁכַּת בֵּית הַחֲלִיפוֹת).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:10:1)


**כְּפוֹרֵי.** מִזְרָקִים, וְנִקְרְאוּ **כְּפוֹרֵי** מִלְּשׁוֹן קִנּוּחַ כְּמוֹ דְּכָפַר יְדֵיהּ בִּגְלִימָא דְּחַבְרֵיהּ (בבא מציעא כד:), עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַמְקַבֵּל דָּם בַּמִּזְרָק הָיָה מְקַנֵּחַ נִצּוֹצוֹת הַדָּם שֶׁנִּתְּזוּ עַל יָדוֹ בִּשְׂפַת הַמִּזְרָק:


**bowls** Heb. כְּפוֹרֵי, bowls, and they were called כְּפוֹרֵי from an expression of wiping, like (Bava Mezia 24b): “who wiped (דְּכָפַר) his hands on his neighbor’s cloak,” because the one who would receive the blood in a bowl would wipe the squirtings of the blood that splattered on his hand on the rim of the bowl.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:10:2)


**מִשְׁנִים.** שְׁנַיִם כֵּלִים אֲחֵרִים שֶׁל מִין אַחֵר:


**secondary** Heb. מִשְּׁנִים, secondary ones, other vessels of another kind.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:11:1)


**כָּל כֵּלִים.** עַתָּה כּוֹלֵל חֶשְׁבּוֹן הַמְּנוּיִין וְשֶׁאֵינָן מְנוּיִין כֻּלָּן בְּמִנְיָן אֶחָד, אֲבָל הַחֲשׁוּבִין הוּא מוֹנֶה:


**All the vessels** Now he totals the sum of those counted and those who were not counted all into one number, but the important ones he counts.


###### Rashi on Ezra 1:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/1:11:2)


**הֵעָלוֹת**. לְשׁוֹן (יִפָּעֵל):


**when the exiles were brought up** Heb. הֵעָלוֹת, [a passive] expression.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:1:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה בְּנֵי הַמְּדִינָה.** אֵלֶּה בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר מִמְּדִינַת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הָעוֹלִים עַתָּה מִן הַשְּׁבִי אֲשֶׁר בַּגּוֹלָה לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**And these are the people of the province** These are the Children of Israel who are from the province of the land of Israel, who are now coming up to Jerusalem from the captivity that is in the exile.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:1:2)


**וַיָּשׁוּבוּ.** וְעַתָּה חָזְרוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**and they returned** And now they returned to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:1:3)


**וִיהוּדָה.** וּבְנֵי יְהוּדָה חָזְרוּ כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד לְעִירוֹ:


**and Judea** And the people of Judea returned, each one to his city.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:2:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ.** בְּנֵי הַמְּדִינָה הַלָּלוּ בַּתְּחִלָּה כַּאֲשֶׁר גָּלוּ מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבָבֶל עִם זְרֻבָּבֶל וְגוֹ׳:


**[Those] who came** these people of the province, in the beginning, when they went into exile from Israel to Babylon with Zerubbabel etc.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:2:2)


**מִסְפָּר.** הָרִאשׁוֹן פּ״א קְמוּצָה בְּגָדוֹל וְשֵׁם אָדָם הוּא, מִסְפַּר הַשֵּׁנִי פּ״א פְּתוּחָה וְדָבוּק הוּא:


**Mispar...the number** Heb. מִסְפָּר. [This word appears twice in this verse.] In the first one, the “pay” is vowelized with a “kamatz,” and it is the name of a man; in the second, the “pay” is vowelized with a “pattah”, and it is the construct state, [meaning the number of].


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:2:3)


**אַנְשֵׁי עַם יִשְׂרָאֵל.** הַלָּלוּ בְּנֵי אָדָם גְּדוֹלִים וַחֲשׁוּבִים הָיוּ, וְהָאֲחֵרִים הָיוּ מְשׁוּכִים אַחֲרֵיהֶם. וְעַל כֵּן מְנָאוּם אַחֲרֵיהֶם לְבַד:


**the important men of the people of Israel** These were the great and important people, and the others followed them. Therefore, they were counted after them separately.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:3:1)


**בְּנֵי פַרְעוֹשׁ וְגוֹ׳.** יֵשׁ בָּהֶם בַּפָּרָשָׁה שֶׁמַּזְכִּיר שֵׁם אֲבִיהֶם עַל שֵׁם בְּנֵי הַמִּשְׁפָּחָה, וְיֵשׁ מֵהֶם שֶׁמַּזְכִּירָם עַל שֵׁם מְקוֹמָם, לוֹמַר בְּנֵי מָקוֹם פְּלוֹנִי:


**The children of Parosh, etc.** There are some in the chapter for whom the father’s name is used for the family name [surname] and there are some who are called by the name of their place, i.e., “the children of such-and-such a place.”



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:36:1)


**הַכֹּהֲנִים בְּנֵי יְדַעְיָה.** עַד עַתָּה מָנָה בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין, וּמֵעַכְשָׁיו הוּא מוֹנֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַעְלָה ״וַיָּקוּמוּ רָאשֵׁי הָאָבוֹת לִיהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין וְהַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם וְגוֹ׳״ (עזרא א:ה):


**The priests: the children of Jedaiah** Until here, he counted the children of Judah and Benjamin, and from now on he counts the priests and the Levites, as he says above (1:5): “So the heads of the families of Judah and Benjamin and the priests and the Levites arose....”



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:41:1)


**הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים.** הַלְוִיִּם שֶׁהָיוּ מְשׁוֹרְרִים, וְכֵן הַשּׁוֹעֲרִים הֵם הַלְוִיִּם שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹעֲרִים מִשַּׁעֲרֵי בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**The singers** The Levites who were singers, and likewise, the gatekeepers are Levites who were gatekeepers of the gates of the Temple.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:43:1)


**הַנְּתִינִים.** עַכְשָׁיו הוּא מוֹנֶה אוֹתָם נְתִינִים שֶׁהָיוּ חוֹטְבֵי עֵצִים וְשׁוֹאֲבֵי מַיִם לָעֵדָה (יהושע ט:כז), וּמִשָּׁם הָיְתָה יוֹצֵאת פַּרְנָסָתָם:


**The Nethinim** Now he counts those Nethinim who were woodcutters and water-drawers for the congregation, and from there they derived their livelihood.



### Verse 58

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:58:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:58:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:58:1)


**וּבְנֵי עַבְדֵי שְׁלֹמֹה.** עֲבָדִים הָיוּ לִשְׁלֹמֹה הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְהַלָּלוּ בָּנִים הָיוּ מִבְּנֵי בְנֵיהֶם:


**and the children of Solomon’s slaves** King Solomon had slaves, and these children were of their descendants.



### Verse 59

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:59:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:59:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:59:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה הָעוֹלִים מִתֵּל מֶלַח וְגוֹ׳.** וְאֵי זֶה מַעֲשֶׂה הִגִּיעַ לָהֶם **וְלֹא יָכְלוּ לְהַגִּיד וְגוֹ׳**:


**And these were the ones who ascended from Tel-Mela, etc.** And what incident happened to them? They could not tell....


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:59:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:59:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:59:2)


**וְזַרְעָם.** שֶׁל אֲבוֹתָם לְפִי שֶׁאָבְדוּ יִחוּס שֶׁלָּהֶם וְלֹא יָדְעוּ **אִם מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל הֵם**:


**and their children** [i.e., the children] of their fathers, because they lost their genealogical records and did not know if they were Israelites.



### Verse 60

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:60:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:60:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:60:1)


**בְּנֵי דְלָיָה וְגוֹ׳.** גַּם הֵם בִּכְלַל אֵלֶּה הָעוֹלִים מִתֵּל מֶלַח (עזרא ב:נט):


**The children of Delaiah** They too are included in “these were the ones who ascended from Tel- Melah.”



### Verse 61

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:61:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:61:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:61:1)


**אֲשֶׁר לָקַח.** כָּל אֶחָד מִבְּנֵי בַרְזִלַּי הַגִּלְעָדִי לָקַח אִשָּׁה:


**who took** Each one of the sons of Barzillai the Gileadite took a wife.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:61:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:61:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:61:2)


**וַיִּקָּרֵא עַל שְׁמָם.** וְהָיָה נִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל בַּרְזִלַּי עַל שְׁמָם, לְפִי שֶׁהֵם לָקְחוּ מִבְּנוֹתָיו:


**and was called by their name** And the name of Barzillai was called on their name because they took of his daughters.



### Verse 62

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:62:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:62:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:62:1)


**אֵלֶּה.** כֹּהֲנִים הַלָּלוּ הַמִּתְיַחֲסִים שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם לְהִתְיַחֵס בְּאוֹתוֹ כְּתָב וְלֹא נִמְצָא כְּתַב יִחוּסָן:


**These** referring to the priests, who had to trace their lineage with that record, and who could not find the record of their lineage.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:62:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:62:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:62:2)


**וַיְגֹאֲלוּ.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְגֹאָלִים וּמְגֹעָלִים שֶׁלֹּא יַעַבְדוּ עוֹד עִם שְׁאָר הַכֹּהֲנִים בַּעֲבוּר שֶׁלֹּא נִמְצָא כְתַב יִחוּסָן:


**and they were disqualified** Heb. וַיְגאַלוּ, for they were defiled and purged from the priesthood that they should no longer perform the service with the remaining priests, because their genealogical record was not found.



### Verse 63

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:63:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:63:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:63:1)


**הַתִּרְשָׁתָא.** הוּא נְחֶמְיָה בֶּן חֲכַלְיָה (נחמיה ח:ט), וְאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ שֶׁנִּקְרָא שְׁמוֹ **הַתִּרְשָׁתָא** שֶׁהִתִּירוּ לוֹ חֲכָמִים לִשְׁתּוֹת יֵין נֶסֶךְ שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת עַל אֲשֶׁר הָיָה מַשְׁקֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ (ירושלמי קידושין ד,א):


**Hattirshatha** That is Nehemiah, the son of Hachaliah (Neh. 8:9), and our Rabbis said (Yerushalmi Kid. 4:1) that he was named Hattirshatha because the Sages permitted him (הָתֵּר) to drink (שָּׁתָּא) wine of gentiles because he was the king’s butler.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:63:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:63:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:63:2)


**מִקֹּדֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים.** חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת כִּשְׁאָר כֹּהֲנִים:


**of the most holy sacrifices** sin-offerings and guilt-offerings, like the other priests.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:63:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:63:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:63:3)


**עַד עֲמֹד כֹּהֵן וְגוֹ׳.** כְּאָדָם הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵירוֹ עַד יְמוֹת הַמָּשִׁיחַ לֹא יַעֲלֶה דָּבָר זֶה, וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לִפְתּוֹר **עַד עֲמֹד כֹּהֵן לְאוּרִים וְתוּמִּים** בְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי, לְפִי שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּפֶרֶק רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל יוֹמָא (יומא כא:) שֶׁהָיָה בַּיִת שֵׁנִי חָסֵר מֵאוּרִים וְתוּמִּים:


**until a priest arises, etc.** like a person who says to his friend, “Until the Messiah comes, this matter will not come about.” I cannot interpret it to mean: until a priest arises for the Urim and Tumim in the Second Temple, because we find in Yoma, chapter 1 (21b) that the Urim and Tumim were missing in the Second Temple.



### Verse 64

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:64:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:64:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:64:1)


**כָּל הַקָּהָל כְּאֶחָד.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ כֻלָּם בְּיַחַד עִם הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם וְהַנְּתִינִים הָיָה בָּהֶם חֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה, וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל הַמְּנוּיִים לְמַעְלָה הֵם שֶׁל שֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין, וְאוֹתָם אֲשֶׁר נֶחְסְרוּ מֵחֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה שֶׁלֹּא תִמְצָאֵם לְמַעְלָה הֵם הָיוּ מִשְּׁאָר הַשְּׁבָטִים. וְכֵן מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (פרק כט): כָּל הַקָּהָל כְּאֶחָד אַרְבַּע רִבּוֹא וְגוֹ׳, וּבִפְרָטָן אֵינָן אֶלָּא שְׁלֹשִׁים אֶלֶף; שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר אֶלֶף הֵיכָן הֵם? אֶלָּא שֶׁעָלוּ מִשְּׁאָר הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**The entire congregation together** When they were all together with the priests, the Levites and the Nethinim, they amounted to this number; and the Children of Israel counted above were of the tribe of Judah and Benjamin, and those missing from this number, which you will not find above, were of the other tribes. So it is explained in Seder Olam (ch. 29): “‘The entire congregation together was forty-two thousand etc.’ But if you add them up, they are only thirty thousand. Where are the thirteen thousand? They ascended from the other tribes.”



### Verse 65

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:65:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:65:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:65:1)


**מִלְּבַד עַבְדֵיהֶם וְאַמְהֹתֵיהֶם**. שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ בִּכְלַל הַקָּהָל:


**Besides their slaves and their bondwomen** who were not included in the congregation.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:65:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:65:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:65:2)


**וְלָהֶם מְשׁוֹרְרִים וּמְשׁוֹרְרוֹת מָאתָיִם.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹלִים בְּשִׂמְחָה מִבָּבֶל לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, הָיוּ צְרִיכִים לִמְשׁוֹרְרִים וּמְשׁוֹרְרוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהוּ מְטַיְּלוֹת בָּהֶם בְּרֹב שִׂמְחָתָם:


**and they had two hundred male and female singers** because they were going up joyfully from Babylon to the land of Israel, they required male and female singers in order that they should stroll among them in their great joy.



### Verse 68

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:68:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:68:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:68:1)


**הִתְנַדְּבוּ.** הָיוּ מִתְנַדְּבִין לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת:


**donated** They were donating toward the building of the Temple.



### Verse 69

###### Rashi on Ezra 2:69:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:69:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:69:1)


**כְּכֹחָם.** כְּפִי כֹּחָם וּמָמוֹנָם:


**According to their ability** according to their ability and their money.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:69:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:69:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:69:2)


**דַּרְכְּמוֹנִים.** שֵׁם מַטְבֵּעַ שֶׁל זָהָב:


**drachmas** Heb. דַּרְכְּמוֹנִים, the name of a golden coin.


###### Rashi on Ezra 2:69:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:69:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/2:69:3)


**וְכָתְנוֹת כֹּהֲנִים.** לְשָׁרֵת בָּהֶם:


**and priests’ tunics** in which to serve.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:1:1)


**וַיִּגַּע.** כְּמוֹ וְהִגִּיעַ:


**arrived** Heb. וַיִגַע like וְהִגִיעַ, it reached.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:1:2)


**הַחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִי.** הוּא חֹדֶשׁ תִּשְׁרֵי וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ בֶּעָרִים מַה עָשׂוּ וַיֵּאָסְפוּ כֻלָּם כְּאֶחָד לִירוּשָׁלִָם:


**The seventh month** That is the month of Tishri, and the Children of Israel who were in the cities, what did they do? They all gathered as one to Jerusalem.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:3:1)


**וַיָּכִינוּ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ עַל מְכוֹנוֹתָיו.** כְּדֵי לְהַקְרִיב עָלָיו קָרְבָּנוֹת, שֶׁהֲרֵי הָיוּ יְרֵאִים מֵעַמֵּי הָאֲרָצוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יְקַנְתְּרוּם וְשֶׁלֹּא יַלְשִׁינוּם לַמֶּלֶךְ. וּבָנוּ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ לַעֲלוֹת עָלָיו עוֹלוֹת לְמַעַן יִשְׁמְעוּ וְיָבִינוּ הָעַמִּים בַּדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂהוּ עַל פִּי הַמֶּלֶךְ וְעַל כֵּן יִמָּנְעוּ מִלְּקַנְתְּרָם לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת:


**And they set the altar on its bases** in order to offer up sacrifices upon it, because they were afraid of the peoples of the land, lest they taunt them and lest they slander them to the king. And they built the altar to offer up burnt offerings upon it, in order that the peoples hear and understand the matter, that they did it with the sanction of the king and therefore they would hesitate to taunt them about the building of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:3:2)


**לַבֹּקֶר וְלָעָרֶב.** תָּמִיד שֶׁל שַׁחַר וְתָמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם (במדבר כח:ד):


**for the morning and for the evening** the daily sacrifice of the morning and the daily sacrifice of twilight.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:4:1)


**וְעֹלַת יוֹם בְּיוֹם בְּמִסְפָּר.** קָרְבְּנוֹת הֶחָג בְּמִסְפָּרָם כְּמִשְׁפָּטָם:


**and the burnt offering of each day in its day, in the number** the sacrifices of the Festivals in their number, according to their ordinance.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן.** וּלְאַחַר הַסֻּכּוֹת הָיוּ מַקְרִיבִים קָרְבָּנוֹת בְּכָל יוֹם לַבֹּקֶר וְלָעֶרֶב:


**And afterwards** And after Succoth they would offer up sacrifices every day, for the morning and for the evening.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:2)


**וְלֶחֳדָשִׁים.** וּלְרָאשֵׁי חֳדָשִׁים הָיוּ מַקְרִיבִים קָרְבָּנוֹת שֶׁל רָאשֵׁי חֳדָשִׁים:


**and for the New Moon** lit. and for the months. And at the beginning of the months they would offer up the sacrifices of the New Moon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:3)


**וּלְכָל מוֹעֲדֵי ה׳.** וְלִשְׁאָר מוֹעֲדוֹת קָרְבְּנֵיהֶם:


**and for all the appointed seasons of the Lord** and for the rest of the appointed seasons of their sacrifices.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:5:4)


**וּלְכָל מִתְנַדֵּב**. וּלְכָל אוֹתָם הַמִּתְנַדְּבִים נִדְבוֹתֵיהֶם הָיוּ מְקַבְּלִים הַכֹּהֲנִים אוֹתָם לְהַקְרִיבָם, וּמִיּוֹם אֶחָד בְּתִשְׁרֵי הִתְחִילוּ לְהַקְרִיב קָרְבָּנוֹת וַעֲדַיִן לֹא נִבְנָה יְסוֹד הַבַּיִת:


**and for everyone who willingly offered** And for all those who willingly offered their free-will offerings, the priests would accept them to sacrifice them, and from the first day of Tishri they commenced to offer up sacrifices, but the foundation of the Temple was not yet built.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:7:1)


**לַצִּידוֹנִים וְלַצּוֹרִים.** בְּנֵי צוֹר וְצִידוֹן שֶׁהָיוּ אוּמָּנִים לִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת (מלכים א ה:טו-לב):


**to the Zidonians and to the Tyrians** the people of Tyre and Zidon, who were the artisans who built the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:7:2)


**אֶל יָם יָפוֹא.** דֶּרֶךְ הַיָּם הָיוּ מְבִיאִים אוֹתָם:


**to the sea of Jaffa** By way of the sea they would bring them.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:7:3)


**כְּרִשְׁיוֹן.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הָיָה רְשׁוּת הַמֶּלֶךְ עֲלֵיהֶם לִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת, כְּרִשְׁיוֹן לְשׁוֹן רְשׁוּת מִגִּזְרַת רָשָׁה שֶׁהֲרֵי מִן אָבָה יֹאמַר אֶבְיוֹן (דברים טו:ד) וּמִן צָבָה יֹאמַר צִבְיוֹן וּמִן חָזָה חִזָּיוֹן (ישעיהו כב:א) וּמִן רָעָה רַעְיוֹן (קהלת א:יז) מִן חָבָה חֶבְיוֹן (חבקוק ג:ד) וּמִן רָשָׁה יֹאמַר רְשׁוּת כְּמוֹ מִן עָנָה עֱנוּת (תהלים כב:כה) וּמִן שָׁבָה שְׁבוּת (יחזקאל כט:יד) וּמִן רָאָה רְאוּת (קהלת ה:י) וּמִן זָנָה זְנוּת (הושע ד:יא) נִמְצְאוּ רְשׁוּת וְרִשְׁיוֹן בָּאִין וְיוֹצְאִין מִגִּזְרָה אַחַת לְפִי שַׁעַר הַדִּקְדּוּק וְלָשׁוֹן אֶחָד הֵם, וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן אֲרֶשֶׁת שְׂפָתֵינוּ (תהלים כא:ג) וְלֹא דִקְדֵּק בְּגִזְרָתוֹ וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן שְׁנֵי פָנִים הַלָּלוּ קְרוֹבִים זֶה לָזֶה בְּמַשְׁמָעוּתָם:


**according to the sanction of** Heb. כְּרִשְׁיוֹן as the sanction of the king was upon them to build the Temple. כְּרִשְׁיוֹן is an expression of permission (רְשּׁוּת) derived from רשּׁה ; since from אבה to want, he says [i.e., derives] אֶבְיוֹן, a needy person; and from צבה, to desire, צִבְיוֹן, desire; and from חזה to see, חִזָיוֹן, a vision; and from רעה, רַעְיוֹן, thought; from חבה, חֶבְיוֹן, hiding; and from רשּׁה, he says רְשּׁוּת, permission; like from, ענה, עֱנוּת, affliction; and from שּׁבה, שְּׁבוּת,captivity; and from ראה, רְאוּת, a sight; and from זנה, זְנוּת, harlotry. The result is that רְשּׁוּת and רִשְׁיוֹן are derived from the same root according to the gauge of grammar, and they are the same expression. Menahem, (p. 34) however, associated it with (Ps. 21:3): “and the speech of his lips (וַַַַַאֲרֶשֶּׁת),” but he was not exact with its root. Nevertheless, these two manners [of defining words] are close to each other in their meanings.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:1)


**בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי.** הוּא חֹדֶשׁ אִיָּר:


**in the second month** That is the month of Iyar.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:2)


**מֵהַשְּׁבִי.** כָּל אוֹתָן שֶׁבָּאוּ מֵהַשִּׁבְיָה לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**from the captivity** all those who came from the captivity to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:3)


**וַיַּעֲמִידוּ.** הֶעֱמִידוּ הַלְוִיִּם הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים לְנַצֵּחַ וְלוֹמַר שִׁירָה לִפְנֵי הַבּוֹנִים בִּשְׁעַת בִּנְיַן יְסוֹד הַבַּיִת:


**and they stationed** They stationed the Levite singers to harmonize and to recite a song before the builders at the time of the building of the foundation of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:8:4)


**לְנַצֵּחַ.** שְׁנֵי נִצּוּחוֹת הַלָּלוּ הֵם נִצּוּחֵי שִׁירָה, כְּמוֹ ״לַמְנַצֵּחַ (בִּנְגִינוֹת) מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד״ (תהילים ד:א):


**to conduct** Heb. לְנַצֵחַ. These two expressions of נִצוּחַ are harmonies of song, like (Ps. 4:1): “To the conductor (לַּמְנַצֵחַ), a song of David.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:9:1)


**וַיַּעֲמֹד יֵשׁוּעַ.** עָמְדוּ וְאָמְרוּ שִׁירָה בְּיַחַד סָבִיב עוֹשֵׂי מְלָאכַת יְסוֹד הַבַּיִת:


**And Jeshua...stood** They stood and recited the song together around the ones who did the work of the foundation of the Temple.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:1)


**וְיִסְּדוּ.** מִשְׁקָל חָזָק מִן יְסוֹד כְּמוֹ ״וְיִשְּׁבוּ טִירוֹתֵיהֶם״ (יחזקאל כה:ד), כֵּן וְיִשְּׁבוּ מִן יָשַׁב, **וְיִסְּדוּ** מִן יָסַד, וּמַשְׁמָעוּתָן **וְיִסְּדוּ** וַיִּשְׁבוּ, בְּמִשְׁקָל חָזָק בִּשְׁתֵּי יוֹדִי״ן אַחַת פְּעֻלָּה וְאַחַת שֶׁל יְסוֹד וּפִתְרוֹנָן הָיוּ מְיַסְּדִים מְיַשְּׁבִים:


**And...laid the foundation** וְיִסְדוּ This is the strong conjugation of יְסוֹד, like (Ezek. 25:4): “and they will settle (וְיִשְּׁבוּ) their palaces.” Just as וְיִשְּׁבוּ is derived from ישּׁב so is וְיִסְדוּ derived from יסד, and their meaning is, וְיִסְדוּ וְיִשְּׁבוּ in the intensive conjugation with two “yud”s, one for the [continuous] action and one for the radical, and their meaning is that they were laying the foundation, they were settling.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:2)


**וְיִסְּדוּ הַבּוֹנִים.** וְהַבּוֹנִים מְיַסְּדִים אֶת יְסוֹד הֵיכַל ה׳, בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהָיוּ מְנַצְּחִים בְּשִׁירָתָם וְהֶעֱמִידוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים מְלֻבָּשִׁים בִּגְדֵי כָבוֹד וַחֲצוֹצְרוֹת בִּידֵיהֶם לִתְקֹעַ בָּהֶם:


**And the builders laid the foundation** And the builders were laying the foundation [see above] of the Temple of the Lord at the time [that] they were harmonizing in their song, and they stationed the priests attired with elegant garments with trumpets in their hands to sound them.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:3)


**וְהַלְוִיִּם בְּנֵי אָסָף.** הָיוּ מְשׁוֹרְרִים:


**and the Levites the sons of Asaph** were the singers.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:4)


**בַּמְצִלְתַּיִם.** כְּמוֹ (תהלים קנ:ה) ״צִלְצְלֵי שָׁמַע״. כְּלֵי שִׁיר הֵם:


**with cymbals** Heb. בַּמְצִלְתַּיִם, like (Ps. 150:9): “with loud sounding cymbals (בְּצִלְצְלֵי שָּׁמַע).” They are musical instruments.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:10:5)


**עַל יְדֵי דָוִד.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְנַצְּחִים וּמְשׁוֹרְרִים שִׁירוֹת וְהוֹדָיוֹת שֶׁל דָּוִד מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**through David** for they were harmonizing and singing songs of David, the king of Israel.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:1)


**וַיַּעֲנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן עֲנִיַּת זֶמֶר, כְּמוֹ ״וַתַּעַן לָהֶם מִרְיָם״ (שמות טו:כא):


**And they sang aloud** Heb. וַיַעֲנוּ, an expression of raising the voice in song, like (Exod. 15: 21): “And Miriam raised her voice in song to them.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:2)


**וּבְהוֹדוֹת.** וּבְהוֹדָיוֹת:


**and with thanks** and with thanksgivings.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:3)


**כִּי טוֹב.** לְהוֹדוֹת לַה׳:


**for it is good** to give thanks to the Lord.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:4)


**וְכָל הָעָם.** שְׁאָר עַם יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and the entire people** the rest of the people of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:11:5)


**עַל הוּסַד.** כָּל הַשְּׂמָחוֹת הַלָּלוּ הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים עַל בִּנְיַן הַיְּסוֹד אֲשֶׁר הוּסַד בְּבֵית ה׳:


**because...was laid** They acted with all these rejoicings because of the building of the foundation, which was laid in the House of the Lord.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:12:1)


**בְּיָסְדוֹ.** בִּיסוֹד שֶׁלּוֹ מִן שֵׁם דָּבָר שֶׁל יוֹסַד, וּמִן יָסַד יֹאמַר **בְּיָסְדוֹ**, כְּמוֹ מִן חֹדֶשׁ ״בְּחָדְשׁוֹ״, מִן קֹדֶשׁ ״בְּקָדְשׁוֹ״:


**when its foundation was laid** Heb. בְּיָסְדוֹ, with its foundation. With the noun of יוֹסֶד and from יסד, he says בְּיָסְדוֹ, like from חדֶשּׁ, בְּחָדְשּׁוֹ, and from קדֶשּׁ, בְּקָדְשּׁוֹ.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:12:2)


**זֶה הַבַּיִת.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ רוֹאִין בִּנְיַן בַּיִת זֶה הָיוּ בוֹכִין, מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהָיוּ זוֹכְרִים אוֹתוֹ בִּנְיָן גָּדוֹל שֶׁל **בַּיִת רִאשׁוֹן**:


**this Temple** When they would see the building of this Temple, they would weep because they remembered the large building of the First Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 3:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:12:3)


**וְרַבִּים.** אֲשֶׁר לֹא רָאוּ בִּנְיַן בַּיִת רִאשׁוֹן הָיוּ שְׂמֵחִין וּמְרִיעִין בְּשִׂמְחָה בְּקוֹל גָּדוֹל מֵרֹב שִׂמְחָתָן שֶׁיָּצְאוּ מִגָּלוּתָן:


**and many** who had not seen the building of the First Temple, were rejoicing and shouting for joy with a loud voice, out of their great joy that they had emerged from their exile.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/3:13:1)


**וְאֵין הָעָם מַכִּירִים.** אוֹתָן הַשּׁוֹמְעִים לֹא הָיוּ מַכִּירִים קוֹל תְּרוּעָה שֶׁל שִׂמְחָה מִפְּנֵי קוֹל בְּכִי הָעָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הָעָם הַשְּׂמֵחִים הָיוּ מְרִיעִים תְּרוּעָה גְדוֹלָה, וְקוֹל הַבְּכִי נִשְׁמַע יוֹתֵר וְיוֹתֵר עַד לְמֵרָחוֹק:


**And the people did not recognize** Those listeners did not recognize the voice of the shout of joy, because of the voice of the people’s weeping, for the rejoicing people were shouting with a great shout, and the voice of weeping was heard farther and farther away.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:1:1)


**צָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין.** הֵם הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר הוֹשִׁיב סַנְחֵרִיב בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים ב יז:כד): ״וַיָּבֵא מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר מִבָּבֶל וּמִכּוּתָה וּמֵעַוָּא וּמֵחֲמָת וּסְפַרְוָיִם, וַיּוֹשֶׁב אֹתָם בְּעָרֵי שֹׁמְרוֹן תַּחַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל״:


**the adversaries of Judah and Benjamin** They are the nations whom Sannecherib settled in the land Israel, as it says (II Kings 17:24): “And the king of Assyria brought [people] from Babylonia and from Cuthah and from Avvah and from Hamath and from Sepharvaim, and he settled them in the cities of Samaria instead of the Children of Israel.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:2:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ לָהֶם נִבְנֶה עִמָּכֶם.** הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים כָּךְ לְמַעַן תִּתְבַּטֵּל מְלֶאכֶת הַבַּיִת עַל יְדֵיהֶם שֶׁלֹּא לִבְנוֹת עוֹד:


**and said to them, “Let us build with you...”** They said this in order that through them the work of the Temple should be disrupted, that they should build no more.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:2:2)


**מִימֵי אֵסַר חַדֹּן מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר.** בְּנוֹ שֶׁל סַנְחֵרִיב, שֶׁלְּאַחַר שֶׁסַּנְחֵרִיב הוֹשִׁיבָם שָׁם הֲרָגוּהוּ אַדְרַמֶּלֶךְ וְשַׂרְאֶצֶר בָּנָיו, וַיִּמְלֹךְ אֵסַר חַדֹּן בְּנוֹ תַּחְתָּיו. שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ישעיה לז:לח): ״וַיְהִי הוּא מִשְׁתַּחֲוֶה בֵּית נִסְרֹךְ אֱלֹהָיו וְאַדְרַמֶּלֶךְ וְשַׂרְאֶצֶר בָּנָיו הִכֻּהוּ בַחֶרֶב וְגוֹמֵר וַיִּמְלֹךְ אֵסַר חַדֹּן בְּנוֹ תַּחְתָּיו״:


**since the days of Esarhaddon the king of Assyria** the son of Sannecherib, for after Sannecherib settled them there, as it says (II Kings 19:37, Isa. 37:38): “And he was prostrating himself in the temple of Nisroch his god, and Adramelech and Sharezer, his sons, slew him by the sword etc., and his son Esarhaddon reigned in his stead.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:3:1)


**כִּי אֲנַחְנוּ.** אֶלָּא אֲנַחְנוּ לְבַדֵּנוּ נִבְנֶה כִּי זֶה מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּמְקוֹם אֶלָּא:


**but we** Heb. כִּי, but we alone shall build. This כִּי serves in place of אֶלָּא, but.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:4:1)


**עַם הָאָרֶץ.** הֵם צָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין:


**the people of the land** They are the adversaries of Judah and Benjamin.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:4:2)


**מְרַפִּים.** לְבַטְּלָם מִמְּלַאכְתָּם:


**were hindering** to disrupt them from their work.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:4:3)


**לִבְנוֹת.** כְּמוֹ מִלִּבְנוֹת, וְכֵן ״עַד כִּי חָדַל לִסְפֹּר״ (בראשית מא:מט):


**from building** Heb. לִבְנוֹת, lit. to build, and so (Gen. 41:49): “until one ceased to count (לִסְפֹּר).”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:5:1)


**וְסוֹכְרִים.** כְּתִיב בְּסָמֶ״ךְ וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ כְּאִלּוּ כָּתוּב בְּשִׂי״ן שֶׁהָיוּ שׂוֹכְרִים יוֹעֲצִים כְּדֵי לְבַטֵּל הַמְּלָאכָה:


**And they would hire** Heb. וְסֹכְרִים. This is written with a “sammech,” but its meaning is as though it was written with a “sin,” [meaning] that they would hire advisors to disrupt the work.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:5:2)


**כָּל יְמֵי.** מַלְכוּת כּוֹרֶשׁ וּמַלְכוּת אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ אֲשֶׁר מָלַךְ אַחַר כּוֹרֶשׁ, עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ שֶׁמָּלַךְ אַחַר אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ, הָיְתָה הַמְּלָאכָה בְּטֵלָה:


**all the days of** the reign of Cyrus and the reign of Ahasuerus, who reigned after Cyrus, until the second year of Darius, who reigned after Ahasuerus, the work was stopped.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:6:1)


**וּבְמַלְכוּת אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ.** שֶׁמָּלַךְ אַחַר כּוֹרֶשׁ הוּא אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ שֶׁלָּקַח אֶסְתֵּר (מגילה יא.):


**And in the reign of Ahasuerus** who reigned after Cyrus; he is the Ahasuerus who took Esther.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:6:2)


**כָּתְבוּ שִׂטְנָה.** שֶׁלֹּא לִבְנוֹת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**they wrote an accusation** not to build the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:6:3)


**עַל יוֹשְׁבֵי יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִָם.** לְהַלְשִׁינָם וּלְקַנְתְּרָם שֶׁלֹּא יִבְנוּ הַבַּיִת:


**against the dwellers of Judea and Jerusalem** to inform on them and to chide them not to build the Temple.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:1)


**וּבִימֵי אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא.** הוּא כּוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס. מִנְיַן אוֹתִיּוֹת שֶׁל כּוֹרֶשׁ עוֹלֶה לְמִנְיַן אוֹתִיּוֹת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ. וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה (ראש השנה ג:): הוּא כּוֹרֶשׁ, הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ, הוּא אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא. כּוֹרֶשׁ עַל שֵׁם שֶׁמֶּלֶךְ כָּשֵׁר הָיָה, אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא עַל שֵׁם הַמַּלְכוּת. וּבְסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה ל) מָצָאתִי: דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הוּא אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא, וְכָל הַמַּלְכוּת כֻּלָּהּ נִקְרֵאת אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא וְכוּ׳:


**And in the days of Artaxerxes** He is Cyrus, the king of Persia. The numerical value of the letters of כּרֶשּׁ equal the numerical value of the letters of דָּרְיָוֶשּׁ: [כ = 20; ר = 200; שּׁ = 300; total = 520. ד = 4; ר = 200; י = 10; 520 ו = 6; שּׁ = 300; total = 520.] And so we learned in Tractate Rosh Hashanah (3b): “The same one is Cyrus, who is Darius, who is Artaxerxes.” [He was called Cyrus כּרֶשּׁ because he was a worthy (כָּשֵּׁר) king; Artaxerxes, because of the kingdom, and in Seder Olam (ch. 30): “Darius was Artaxerxes (אַרְתַּחְשַּׁשְּׁתָּא), and the entire kingdom was called Artaxerxes, etc.” [meaning that all the kings were called Artaxerxes].


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:2)


**כָּתַב בְּשָׁלֵם.** כָּתַב מִכְתָּבוֹ בִּדְבָרִים שֶׁל שָׁלוֹם:


**wrote with peace** wrote his letter with words of peace.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:3)


**מִתְרְדָת טָבְאֵל.** שֵׁם אָדָם הוּא, מִצָּרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין:


**Mithredath Tabeel** This is the name of a man, one of the adversaries of Judah and Benjamin.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:4)


**וּשְׁאָר כְּנָוֹתָיו.** חֲבֵרוֹתָיו וְסִיעוֹתָיו:


**and the rest of his colleagues** Heb. כְּנָותָיו, his companies and his societies.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:5)


**עַל אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא.** אֶל אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא:


**to Artaxerxes** [as translated,] to Artaxerxes.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:6)


**וּכְתָב הַנִּשְׁתְּוָן.** וּכְתָב הָאִגֶּרֶת:


**and the script of the epistle** Heb. וּכְתָב הַנִשְּׁתְּוָן.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:7)


**כָּתוּב אֲרָמִית.** בְּאוֹתִיּוֹת אֲרָמִית:


**written in Aramaic** in Aramaic characters.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:7:8)


**וּמְתֻרְגָּם אֲרָמִית.** הַמִּכְתָּב הָיָה מְפֹרָשׁ בְּלָשׁוֹן אֲרָמִית:


**and explained in Aramaic** The script was explained in the Aramaic language.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:1)


**רְחוּם בַּעַל טְעֵם.** רְחוּם הָיָה מַזְכִּיר וּבַעַל דְּבָרִים לְסַדֵּר הַמִּכְתָּב:


**Rehum was the secretary** Rehum was the secretary and the master of words to compose the epistle.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:2)


**וְשִׁמְשַׁי סָפְרָא.** וְשִׁמְשַׁי הָיָה סוֹפֵר, הוּא שִׁמְשַׁי בְּנוֹ שֶׁל הָמָן. וְכֵן דֶּרֶךְ הַמִּקְרָא לְהַזְכִּיר סוֹפֵר וּמַזְכִּיר שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּיַחַד, לְפִי שֶׁהֵם זְקוּקִים זֶה לָזֶה, הָאֶחָד מְסַדֵּר וּמַזְכִּירוֹ וְהַסּוֹפֵר כּוֹתֵב, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״אֱלִיחֹרֶף וַאֲחִיָּה בְּנֵי שִׁישָׁא סֹפְרִים יְהוֹשָׁפָט בֶּן אֲחִילוּד הַמַּזְכִּיר״ (מלכים א ד:ג). וּבְמָקוֹם אַחֵר נֶאֱמַר: ״וַיָּבֹא אֶלְיָקִים בֶּן חִלְקִיָּהוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים ב יח:יח) ״וְשֶׁבְנָא הַסֹּפֵר וְיוֹאָח בֶּן אָסָף הַמַּזְכִּיר״:


**and Shimshai was the scribe** And Shimshai was the scribe: he is Shimshai the son of Haman. So it is customary to mention the scribe and the secretary together because they need each other; one composes and dictates, and the scribe writes, as it says (I Kings 4:3): “Eliphoreph and Ahiah, the sons of Shisha, scribes, Jehoshaphat, the son of Ahlud, the secretary,” and in another place it says (Isa. 36: 22): “And Eliakim, the son of Hilkiah etc. and Shebna the scribe, and Joah, the son of Asaph the secretary, came.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:3)


**כָּתְבוּ אִגְּרָא.** הַלָּלוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם **רְחוּם וְשִׁמְשַׁי** כָּתְבוּ הָאִגֶּרֶת כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוָּה אֲלֵיהֶם מִתְרְדָת וְטָבְאֵל, וְכֻלָּם הָיוּ מְיֻשָּׁבִים בְּעָרֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן:


**they wrote a letter** These two, Rehum and Shimshai, wrote the letter as Mithredath Tabeel had commanded them, and they all were dwellers of the cities of Samaria.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:4)


**עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם.** עַל אוֹדוֹת בִּנְיַן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**about Jerusalem** about the building of the Temple, which is in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:8:5)


**כְּנֵימָא.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר, וְהוּא דֻּגְמַת לְשׁוֹן גְּמָרָא כִּדְבָּעִינַן לְמֵימַר קַמָּן:


**as is stated** Aram. כְּנֵמָא, as is stated, and that is similar to the expression of the Gemara: “as we wish to say further.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:1)


**אֱדַיִן.** אָז:


**Then** Aram. אֱדַיִן, equivalent to the Hebrew אָז.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:2)


**רְחוּם.** שֵׁם אָדָם:


**Rehum** the name of a man.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:3)


**בַּעַל טַעַם.** מַזְכִּיר וּבַעַל דְּבָרִים:


**the secretary** Aram. בְּעֵל טְעֵם secretary and master of words.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:4)


**וּשְׁאָר כְּנָוָתְהוֹן.** וּשְׁאָר סִיעוֹתֵיהֶם:


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:5)


**דִּינָיֵא וַאֲפַרְסַתְכָיֵא וְגוֹ׳.** הַלָּלוּ כֻּלָּם שֵׁמוֹת שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁהוֹשִׁיב סַנְחֵרִיב בְּעָרֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן:


**the Dinites, the Apharesattechites, etc.** All these are names of nations whom Sennacherib repatriated in the cities of Samaria.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:6)


**אֲפָרְסָיֵא.** הֵם פַּרְסִיִּים שֶׁהוֹשִׁיב סַנְחֵרִיב בְּעָרֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן (מלכים ב יז:כד):


**the Apharesites** They are the Persians whom Sennacherib settled in the cities of Samaria.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:7)


**אַרְכְּוָי.** הֵם בְּנֵי אֶרֶךְ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וְאֶרֶךְ וְאַכַּד וְכַלְנֵה״ (בראשית י:י):


**the Archevites** They are the people of Erech, as it says (Gen. 10:10): “and Erech and Accad and Calneh.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:8)


**בָּבְלָיֵא.** הֵם בְּנֵי בָבֶל:


**the Babylonians** the people of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:9)


**שׁוּשַׁנְכָיֵא.** בְּנֵי שׁוּשַׁן הַבִּירָה:


**the Shushanchites** the people of Shushan, the capital.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:9:10)


**עֵלְמָיָא.** בְּנֵי עֵילָם. מִכָּל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת הַלָּלוּ הוֹשִׁיב סַנְחֵרִיב בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְכֻלָּן הָיוּ מְשִׁיבִין וּמַסְכִּימִים בִּשְׁלִיחוּת הַמִּכְתָּב זֶה:


**the Elamites** the people of Elam. Sennacherib settled all of these peoples in the land of Israel, and all of them were settling and concurring with the commission of this letter.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:1)


**וּשְׁאָר אוּמַּיָּא.** וּשְׁאָר הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר הִגְלָה סַנְחֵרִיב, כֻּלָּם הִסְכִּימוּ:


**And the rest of the nations** and the rest of the nations that Sennacherib exiled; they have all concurred.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:2)


**אָסְנַפַּר.** סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים ב יח:יג)׃


**Asenappar** Sennacherib.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:3)


**רַבָּא וְיַקִּירָא.** שֶׁהָיָה מֶלֶךְ גָּדוֹל וּמְכֻבָּד, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כֹּה אָמַר הַמֶּלֶךְ הַגָּדוֹל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר״ (ישעיה לו:ד):


**the great and honored** for he was a great and honored king, as it says (Isa. 36:4): “So has the great king, the king of Assyria, said.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:4)


**וְהוֹתֵב הִמּוֹ.** הוֹשִׁיב אוֹתָם:


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:5)


**בְּקִרְיָה דִּי שָׁמְרָיִן.** בֶּעָרִים אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֹת שׁוֹמְרוֹן:


**in the cities of Samaria** in the cities surrounding Samaria.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:6)


**וּשְׁאָר עֲבַר נַהֲרָה.** וּשְׁאָר הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר בְּעֵבֶר הַנָּהָר, לְפִי שֶׁהַנָּהָר נְהַר פְּרָת מַפְסִיק בֵּין אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבָבֶל, נִמְצְאוּ אוֹתָן עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁלְצַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵם בְּעֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְאוֹתָן הָעוֹמְדִים בְּבָבֶל:


**and the rest of the other side of the river** and the rest of the nations that are on the other side of the river; because the river Euphrates intervenes between the land of Israel and Babylon, those nations that are in Israel are on the opposite side of the river of those found in Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:10:7)


**וּכְעֶנֶת.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם וְאַנְשֵׁי כְעֶנֶת כֻּלָּם הָיוּ מַסְכִּימִים בִּשְׁלִיחוּת הַמִּכְתָּב הַזֶּה:


**and Ke’eneth** a name of a place; and all the people of Ke’eneth have concurred with the commission of this letter.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:1)


**דְּנָה פַּרְשֶׁגֶן אִגַּרְתָּא.** וְזֶהוּ פִּתְרוֹן הָאִגֶּרֶת:


**This is the meaning of the letter** and this is the interpretation of the letter.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:2)


**פַּרְשֶׁגֶן.** כְּמוֹ פַּתְשֶׁגֶן הַכְּתָב (אסתר ג:יד):


**the meaning** Aram. פַּרְשֶּׁגֶן like (Esther 3:14): “The meaning of (פַּתְשֶּׁגֶן) the writ.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:3)


**דִּי שְׁלַחוּ עֲלוֹהִי.** אֲשֶׁר שָׁלְחוּ אֵלָיו:


**that they sent to him** [as translated,] that they sent to him.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:4)


**עַל אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתְּא.** אֶל אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתְּא:


**to Artaxerxes** [as translated,] to Artaxerxes.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:11:5)


**עַבְדָךְ אֱנָשׁ עֲבַר נַהֲרָה וּכְעֶנֶת.** עַבְדֶיךָ הֵם אַנְשֵׁי עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר וְאַנְשֵׁי כְעֶנֶת. כָּל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת הַמְּנוּיוֹת כָּאן נִכְלָלוֹת בִּכְלָל זֶה, שֶׁכֻּלָּן הָיוּ לְצַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְאוֹתָם הַשּׁוֹכְנִים בְּבָבֶל. וְזֶהוּ תְּחִלַּת הַמִּכְתָּב: **עַבְדָךְ אֱנָשׁ עֲבַר נַהֲרָה וְגוֹמֵר** עַד **לָא אִיתַי לָךְ**:


**Your servants are the people of the other side of the river and Ke’eneth** The people of the other side of the river and the people of Ke’eneth are your servants. All the nations counted here are included in this generalization, for they were all on the side of the land of Israel, which is on the opposite side of the river of those dwelling in Babylon, and this is the beginning of the letter: “Your servants are the people of the other side of the river etc.” until, “...you will have no part.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:1)


**יְדִיעַ לֶהֱוֵא לְמַלְכָּא.** דָּבָר יָדוּעַ יִהְיֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**Let it be known to the king** Let it be a known thing to the king.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:2)


**דִּי יְהוּדָיֵא.** אֲשֶׁר הַיְּהוּדִים שֶׁעָלוּ מֵאִתְּךָ אֵלֵינוּ בָּאוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**that the Jews** that the Jews who ascended from you upon us have come to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:3)


**קִרְיְתָא מָרָדְתָּא וּבָאִישְׁתָּא בָּנַיִן.** עִיר מוֹרֶדֶת וְחוֹטֵאת הֵם בּוֹנִים:


**the rebellious and sinful city they are building** [as translated,] a rebellious and sinful city they are building.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:4)


**וְשׁוּרַיָּא שַׁכְלִילוּ.** וְהַחוֹמוֹת יִסְּדוּ:


**the walls they have completed** [as translated,] the walls they have completed.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:5)


**וְאֻשַּׁיָּא.** הַכְּתָלִים:


**and the walls** [as translated,] and the walls.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:12:6)


**יְחִיטוּ.** לְשׁוֹן פְּתִיל, כְּמוֹ ״אִם מִחוּט״ (בראשית יד:כג), שֶׁהֵם חוֹפְרִים וּמְחַבְּרִים הַכְּתָלִים זֶה עִם זֶה:


**they have joined** Aram. יָחִיטוּ, an expression of a thread, like (Gen. 14:23): “not from a thread, (מִחוּטּ),” for they are “sewing” and joining the walls one to another.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:1)


**כְּעַן יְדִיעַ.** עַתָּה יָדוּעַ יִהְיֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**Now let it be known** Now let it be known to the king.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:2)


**דִּי הֵן קִרְיְתָא דָךְ תִּתְבְּנֵא.** אֲשֶׁר אִם הָעִיר הַזֹּאת תִּבָּנֶה:


**that if this city is built** [as translated,] that if this city is built.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:3)


**דַּךְ.** זֹאת:


**this** [as translated,] this.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:4)


**וְשׁוּרַיָּא יִשְׁתַּכְלְלוּן.** וְהַחוֹמוֹת יִהְיוּ מְיֻסָּדוֹת׃


**and the walls are founded** [as translated,] and the walls are founded.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:5)


**מִנְדָּה בְלוֹ וַהֲלָךְ.** מִינֵי מִסִּים הֵם וְכֶסֶף גֻּלְגֹּלֶת:


**the king’s due, the head tax, or the meal tax** They are types of taxes and the money for the head taxes.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:6)


**לֹא יִנְתְּנוּן.** לֹא יִתְּנוּ עוֹד מַס:


**they will not give** They will no longer pay taxes.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:13:7)


**וְאַפְתֹּם מְלָכִים תְּהַנְזִיק.** וּמַס הַמְּלָכִים תֻּזַּק שֶׁלֹּא יִתְּנוּ עוֹד מַס לַמְּלָכִים:


**and the tax of the kings will suffer** [as translated,] and the tax of the kings will suffer, for they will no longer give tax to the kings.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:1)


**כְּעַן כָּל קֳבֵל דִּי מְלַח הֵיכְלָא מְלַחְנָא.** עַתָּה כָּל כְּנֶגֶד דָּבָר זֶה אֲשֶׁר חֻרְבַּן הַהֵיכָל אָנוּ רוֹצִים לְהַחֲרִיב, **דִּי מְלַח** לְשׁוֹן חוּרְבָּן וְשִׁמָּמוֹן כְּמוֹ ״אֶרֶץ מְלֵחָה וְלֹא תֵשֵׁב״ (ירמיה יז:ו):


**Now in view of this, that we wish to destroy the Temple** Now in view of this matter, that we wish to implement the destruction of the Temple. דִי מְלַח is an expression of destruction and desolation, like Jer. 17:6): “barren (מְלֵחָה) land that is uninhabitable.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:2)


**מְלַחְנָא.** אָנוּ רוֹצִים לִסְתּוֹר וּלְהַחֲרִיב:


**we wish to destroy** We wish to demolish and to destroy.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:3)


**וְעַרְוַת מַלְכָּא.** וּבִזְיוֹן הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and... the king’s disgrace** Aram. וְעַרְוַת מַלְכָּא, lit. and the king’s nakedness.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:4)


**לָא אֲרִיךְ.** אֵינוֹ הָגוּן לָנוּ לִרְאוֹת, אֲרִיךְ הָגוּן כְּמוֹ ״אֲרִיךְ אוֹ לָא אֲרִיךְ״ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סֻכָּה:


**it is improper** Aram. לָא אַרִיךְ, it is improper for us to see. אַרִיךְ means proper, like “Is it proper (אַרִיךְ) or improper (לָא אַרִיךְ)?” in Tractate Succah (44b).


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:14:5)


**עַל דְּנָה.** עַל זֹאת שָׁלַחְנוּ וְהוֹדַעְנוּ לַמֶּלֶךְ׃


**therefore** therefore we have sent and notified the king.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:1)


**דִּי יְבַקַּר.** אֲשֶׁר יְבַקֵּר וְיִדְרֹשׁ הַדּוֹרֵשׁ בְּסֵפֶר הַזִּכְרוֹנוֹת שֶׁל אֲבוֹתֶיךָ הַמְּלָכִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים:


**That one should search** that a seeker should search and seek in the annals of your fathers, the first kings.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:2)


**וְתִהַשְׁכַּח.** וְתִמְצָא בְּסֵפֶר הַזִּכְרוֹנוֹת וְתֵדַע כִּי הָעִיר הַזֹּאת עִיר מוֹרֶדֶת וּמַזֶּקֶת הַמְּלָכִים וְהַמְּדִינוֹת׃


**and you will find** And you will find in the annals, and you will know that this city is a rebellious city, and it injures the kings and the countries.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:3)


**וְאֶשְׁתַּדּוּר עָבְדִין בְּגַוַּהּ.** וּמֶרֶד הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים יוֹשְׁבֶיהָ בְּתוֹכָהּ, שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מוֹרְדִין בְּמַלְכֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**and they have made rebellion in its midst** And its inhabitants were making rebellion in its midst, for Israel would rebel against the kings of the nations.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:4)


**מִן יוֹמָת עָלְמָא.** מִימוֹת הָעוֹלָם, מִיָּמִים קַדְמוֹנִים, כָּךְ הָיָה מִנְהָגָם לִמְרוֹד בְּמַלְכֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**since days of yore** Aram. מִן יוֹמָת עָלְמָא, from days of yore. Since ancient times, it has been their custom to rebel against the kings of the nations.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:15:5)


**עַל דְּנָה.** עַל כָּךְ הָיְתָה הָעִיר הַזֹּאת חֲרֵבָה:


**because of this** Because of this, this city was destroyed.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:1)


**מְהוֹדְעִין אֲנַחְנָה לְמַלְכָּא.** מוֹדִיעִין אֲנַחְנָא לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**We make known to the king** [as translated,] we make known to the king.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:2)


**דִּי הֵן קִרְיְתָא דָךְ.** אֲשֶׁר אִם הָעִיר הַזֹּאת תִּבָּנֶה וְחוֹמוֹתֶיהָ מְיֻסָּדִין:


**that if this city** that if this city is built, and its walls are founded.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:3)


**לָקֳבֵל דְּנָה.** כְּנֶגֶד כֵּן וּבִשְׁבִיל כָּךְ אֵין לְךָ חֵלֶק בְּכָל עֲבַר נַהֲרָא, לְפִי שֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל יִהְיוּ מוֹרְדִין בְּךָ וְיִקְחוּ הַכֹּל מִיָּדֶךָ:


**because of this** In view of this and because of this, you will have no part in the entire other side of the river, because Israel will rebel against you and take everything out of your hands.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:16:4)


**בְּעֵבֶר נַהֲרָא.** הִיא כָּל הָאָרֶץ שֶׁל צַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְאוֹתָן שֶׁבְּבָבֶל:


**in the other side of the river** That is the entire area of the side of Israel, which is on the other side of the river of those who are in Babylon.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:17:1)


**פִּתְגָמָא שְׁלַח מַלְכָּא.** דָּבָר שָׁלַח הַמֶּלֶךְ׃


**The king sent a word** Aram. פִּתְגָּמָא שְּׁלַח מַלְכָּא


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:17:2)


**עַל רְחוּם בְּעֵל טְעֵם.** אֶל רְחוּם הַמַּזְכִּיר וְשִׁמְשַׁי הַסּוֹפֵר וּלְשָׁאר סִיעוֹתֵיהֶם הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּעָרֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן, וּלְשָׁאר הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר בְּעֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְצַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**to Rehum the secretary** to Rehum the secretary, and Shimshai the scribe, and to the rest of their companies, who dwelt in the cities of Samaria, and to the rest of the nations who were on the other side of the river of the side of the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:17:3)


**שְׁלָם וּכְעֶת.** מְקוֹמוֹת הֵם, וּכְעֶת כְּמוֹ ״וּכְעֶנֶת״ (עזרא ד:יא), פְּעָמִים קוֹרְהוּ כָּךְ וּפְעָמִים כָּךְ:


**Shelam and Ke’eth** They are places, like “and Ke’eneth.” Sometimes he calls it this and sometimes that.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:18:1)


**נִשְׁתְּוָנָא.** כְּתָב הַנִּשְׁתְּוָן אֲשֶׁר שְׁלַחְתֶּם אֵלֵינוּ הָיָה **מְפֹרָשׁ וְקָרוּי לְפָנַי**׃


**The letter** The writ of the letter that you sent to us was explained and read before me.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:19:1)


**שִׂים טְעֵם.** וּמֵאִתִּי הָיְתָה שִׂימַת דְּבַר צִוּוּי אֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִי:


**the order was given** And the order was given by me, the matter of a command that I commanded.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:19:2)


**וּבַקַּרוּ וְהַשְׁכַּחוּ.** וּבָדְקוּ בְּסֵפֶר הַזִּכְרוֹנוֹת הַמְּלָכִים וּמָצְאוּ כָתוּב אֲשֶׁר הָעִיר הַזֹּאת מִימוֹת הָעוֹלָם הָיְתָה מִתְנַשֵּׂאת וּמִתְרוֹמֶמֶת עַל כָּל מַלְכֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**and they searched and found** And they searched in the annals of the kings and found written that this city, from days of yore, would raise itself up and exalt itself over all the kings of the nations.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:19:3)


**וּמְרַד.** וְסַרְבָנוּת הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים בָּהּ לִמְרוֹד בְּמַלְכֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**and rebellion.** and disobedience were committed there to rebel against the kings of the nations.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:20:1)


**וּמַלְכִין תַּקִּיפִין.** וּמְלָכִים חֲזָקִים הָיוּ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹשְׁלִים וְשַׁלִּיטִים בְּכָל עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר שֶׁל צַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּשְׁלֹמֹה ״כִּי הוּא רֹדֶה בְּכָל עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א ד:ה). וּמִסִּים וְכֶסֶף גֻּלְגֹּלֶת הָיָה נָתוּן לָהֶם, שֶׁהָיוּ הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת מַעֲלִין לָהֶם מַס:
