**And mighty kings** and mighty kings were in Jerusalem who were the rulers and governors over the entire side of the river on the side of Israel, as it says regarding Solomon (I Kings 5:4): “For he had dominion over all this side of the river, etc.” And taxes and the head tax were given to them, for the nations paid them tribute.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:21:1)


**כְּעַן שִׂימוּ טְעֵם.** עַתָּה שִׂימוּ דָבָר לְהַכְרִיז בָּאָרֶץ לְבַטֵּל אֲנָשִׁים הַלָּלוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן הַבִּנְיָן, וְהָעִיר הַזֹּאת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם לֹא תִבָּנֶה עַד אֲשֶׁר מִמֶּנִּי יוּשַׂם הַדָּבָר לִבְנוֹת הָעִיר עַל דַּעְתִּי וּרְשׁוּתִי:


**Now issue an order** Now give out a word to announce in the land to stop these men, the Children of Israel, from the work, and the city of Jerusalem shall not be built until orders are given with my knowledge and my authorization.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:22:1)


**וּזְהִירִין הֱווֹ שָׁלוּ.** וּזְהִירִין תִּהְיוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת דִּחוּי וּשְׁגָגָה, **שָׁלוּ** דשטורביר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And beware of committing an error** And you shall beware of committing any postponement or error. שָּׁלוּ is desto(u)rber in Old French, to violate.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:22:2)


**לְמֶעְבַּד עַל דְּנָה.** לַעֲשׂוֹת עַל כָּךְ:


**of committing...in this matter** [as translated,] of committing in this manner.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:22:3)


**לָמָּה יִשְׂגֵּא.** מַדּוּעַ יִגְדַּל מַעֲשֵׂה הַשְׁחָתָה בָּעִיר הַזֹּאת לְהַזִּיק הַמְּלָכִים. עַד כָּאן תְּשׁוּבַת כּוֹרֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר הֵשִׁיב:


**Why should...increase** Why should the act of destruction in this city increase to injure the kings? Until here is Cyrus’ reply, which he sent back.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:1)


**אֱדַיִן.** אָז:


**then** [as translated,] then.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:2)


**דִּי פַרְשֶׁגֶן.** מִן פִּתְרוֹן כְּתַב הַנִּשְׁתְּוָן שֶׁל אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתְּא הַמֶּלֶךְ הוּא כּוֹרֶשׁ הָיָה הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה:


**since the interpretation** Since the interpretation of the writ of the letter of Artaxerxes the king—he is Cyrus—this incident took place.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:3)


**קְרִי קֳדָם.** שֶׁהָיָה כְּתָב הַנִּשְׁתְּוָן קָרוּי לִפְנֵי רְחוּם וְשִׁמְשַׁי וְסִיעָתֵיהֶם, וְהָלְכוּ בִּמְרוּצָה וּבְבֶהָלָה לִירוּשָׁלַיִם אֶל הַיְּהוּדִים, וּבִטְּלוּ אוֹתָם בִּזְרוֹעַ וְכֹחַ שֶׁלֹּא לִבְנוֹת עוֹד הַבִּנְיָן:


**was read before** for the writ of this letter was read before Rehum and Shimshai and their companies, and they went with speed and haste to Jerusalem, to the Jews, and stopped them with force and might from continuing the construction of the edifice.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:4)


**עַל יְהוּדָיֵא.** אֶל הַיְּהוּדִים:


**to the Jews** Aram. עַל יְהוּדָיֵא, lit. On the Jews.


###### Rashi on Ezra 4:23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:23:5)


**הֵמּוֹ.** הֵם:


**and they** Aram. הִמוֹ, equivalent to הֵם.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezra 4:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/4:24:1)


**בְּאֵדַיִן.** אָז הָיְתָה בְּטֵלָה מְלֶאכֶת בִּנְיַן בֵּית אֱלֹהִים אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם **עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרָס.** שֶׁאַחַר כּוֹרֶשׁ מָלַךְ אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ שֶׁלָּקַח אֶסְתֵּר, וְאַחַר אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ מָלַךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ בְּנוֹ שֶׁל אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ שֶׁהוּא בֶּן אֶסְתֵּר. וּמִשְּׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה, שֶׁהִשְׁלִימוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה לְחָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם. שֶׁהֲרֵי מֵחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת שֶׁגָּלָה צִדְקִיָּהוּ עַד שְׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (פרק כט). וּשְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה מִשְּׁנַת א׳ לְכוֹרֶשׁ עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ, נִמְצְאוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה שְׁלֵמִים. וּבִשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הִתְחִילוּ לִבְנוֹת הַבִּנְיָן עַד שֶׁגְּמָרוּהוּ:


**Then** Then the work of the building of the House of God, which was in Jerusalem, was suspended until the second year of Darius, the king of Persia; for after Cyrus, Ahasuerus, who married Esther, reigned, and after Ahasuerus, Darius, the son of Ahasuerus, who was the son of Esther, reigned. And from the first year of Cyrus, the king of Persia, until the second year of Darius were eighteen years, which completed the seventy years from the destruction of Jerusalem. For from the destruction of the Temple, when Zedekiah was exiled, until the first year of Cyrus, were fifty-two years, as is explained in Seder Olam (ch. 29). There were then eighteen years from the first year of Cyrus until the second year of Darius, totaling a complete seventy years, and in the second year of Darius they commenced to build the Temple until they completed it.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:1:1)


**וְהִתְנַבִּי חַגַּי נְבִיאָה.** וְעַתָּה בִּשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ נִתְנַבְּאוּ חַגַּי וּזְכַרְיָה הַנְּבִיאִים אֶל הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר בְּאֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה וּבִירוּשָׁלַיִם בְּשֵׁם אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲלֵיהֶם לִבְנוֹת בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת בְּלֹא רְשׁוּת הַמֶּלֶךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**Now Haggai the prophet...prophesied** And now in the second year of Darius, there came a prophecy to Haggai and Zechariah, the prophets to the Jews who were in the land of Judea and in Jerusalem, in the Name of the God of Israel to them, to build the edifice of the Temple without the authorization of King Darius.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:1:2)


**עַל יְהוּדָיֵא.** אֶל הַיְּהוּדִים:


**to the Jews** Aram. עָל יְהוּדָיֵא lit. On the Jews.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:1:3)


**עֲלֵיהוֹן.** אֲלֵיהֶם:


**to them** Aram. עֲלֵיהוֹן, lit. on them.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:2:1)


**בְּאֵדַיִן.** אָז:


**Then** [as translated,] then.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:2:2)


**וְשָׂרָיו.** הִתְחִילוּ לִבְנוֹת בֵּיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְעִמָּהֶם הַנְּבִיאִים שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עוֹזְרִין וּמְסַיְּעִין לָהֶם:


**and commenced** They began to build the House of the Holy One, blessed be He, which is in Jerusalem, and with them were the prophets of the Holy One, blessed be He, helping and assisting them.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:1)


**בֵּהּ זִמְנָא אֲתָה עֲלֵיהוֹן.** בְּאוֹתוֹ הַזְּמַן בָּאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם הַלָּלוּ צָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין לְקַנְתֵּר וּלְהַלְשִׁין עֲלֵיהֶם עַל עֵסֶק הַבִּנְיָן:


**At that time, there came upon them** At that time, there came upon them these adversaries of Judah and Benjamin to accuse and to slander them regarding the matter of the building.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:2)


**פַּחַת עֲבַר נַהֲרָה.** שִׁלְטוֹן שֶׁל עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר:


**the governor of the other side of the river** [as translated,] the governor of the other side of the river.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:3)


**וּכְנָוָתְהוֹן.** וְסִיעָתְהוֹן:


**and their companies** [as translated,] and their companies.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:4)


**וְכֵן אָמְרִין לְהוֹם.** וְכֵן הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים לָהֶם:


**and so they said to them** [as translated,] and so they said to them.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:5)


**מַן שָׂם.** מִי צִוָּה לָכֶם דְּבַר הַבַּיִת לִבְנוֹת?


**Who issued to you** Who ordered you, regarding the matter of building the Temple?


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:3:6)


**וְאֻשַּׁרְנָא דְנָה לְשַׁכְלָלָה.** הַכְּתָלִים הָאֵלֶּה לְיַסֵּד, כְּמוֹ ״וְאֻשַּׁיָּא יְחִיטוּ״ (עזרא ד:יב), וּבִנְיַן חוֹמוֹת לְבֵנִים. כָּךְ חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**and to found these walls** Aram. וְאֻשַּׁרְנָא, and to found these walls, like (4:12): “and the walls (וְאֻשַּׁיָא) they have joined,” the building of walls of bricks (לְבֵנִים) so did Menahem (p. 36) associate it.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:4:1

**אֱדַיִן.** אָז:


**Then** [as translated,] then.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:2)


**כְּנֵמָא.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר:


**as we will say** [as translated,] as we will say.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:3)


**אֲמַרְנָא לְהוֹם.** אָמְרוּ לָהֶם לְיִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹתָן צָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין:


**they said to them** Those adversaries of Judah and Benjamin said to them [i.e.,] to Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:4)


**מַן אִינּוּן.** מִי הֵם שְׁמוֹת הָאֲנָשִׁים?


**What are** What are the names of the men?


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:4:5)


**דִּי דְנָה.** אֲשֶׁר זֶה הַבִּנְיָן הֵם בּוֹנִים:


**who...this** who are building this building.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:1)


**וְעֵין אֱלָהֲהוֹם.** וְעֵינוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא אֱלֹהֵיהֶם הָיְתָה עַל זִקְנֵי הַיְּהוּדִים לְהַעֲלוֹתָם בִּמְלֶאכֶת הַבִּנְיָן:


**And the eye of their God** And the eye of the Holy One, blessed be He, Who is their God, was on the elders of the Jews to upraise them (sic) in the work of the building.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:2)


**וְלָא בַטִּלוּ הִמּוֹ.** וְלֹא בִּטְּלוּ הֵם אֶת הַבּוֹנִים:


**and they did not stop them** And they did not stop the builders.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:3)


**עַד טַעְמָא לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ יְהָךְ.** וְהֵשִׁיבוּ זִקְנֵי הַיְּהוּדִים דָּבָר לְאוֹתָן הַצָּרִים שֶׁלֹּא יְבַטְּלוּ אֶת הַבִּנְיָן עַד אֲשֶׁר יֵלֵךְ הַדָּבָר לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**until the matter came to Darius** And the elders of the Jews answered a word to those adversaries that they should not stop the building until the matter would go to King Darius.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:5:4)


**וֶאֱדַיִן יְתִיבוּן.** וְאָז יָשִׁיבוּ הַיְּהוּדִים כְּתַב הַנִּשְׁתְּוָן עַל כָּךְ, עַל תְּשׁוּבָתוֹ שֶׁל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**and then they would answer** And then the Jews would answer the writ of the letter concerning this, concerning Darius’ reply.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:6:1)


**פַּרְשֶׁגֶן.** פִּתְרוֹן פַּתְשֶׁגֶן הָאִגֶּרֶת:


**The interpretation** the interpretation of the text of the letter.

**וּכְנָוֹתֵהּ.** וְסִיעוֹתָיו:


**and his companies** [as translated,] and his companies.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:6:3)


**אֲפַרְסְכָיֵא.** אֻמָּה אַחַת הֵן שֶׁהָיוּ בְּסִיעוֹתֵיהֶם:


**the Apharsechites** They are one nation that was in their companies.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:6:4)


**עַל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ מַלְכָּא.** אֶל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**to King Darius** Aram. עַל דָּרְיָוֶשּׁ מַלְכָּא, lit. on King Darius.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:7:1)


**עֲלוֹהִי.** אֵלָיו:


**to him** Aram. עֲלוֹהִי, lit. on him.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:7:2)


**וְכִדְנָה כְּתִיב בְּגַוֵּהּ.** וְכָךְ הָיָה כָתוּב בְּתוֹכָהּ שֶׁל אִגֶּרֶת:


**and so was written in it** and so was written in the letter.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:7:3

**שְׁלָמָא כֹּלָּא.** כָּל הַשָּׁלוֹם יִהְיֶה לוֹ:


**all peace** All peace shall be his.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:1)


**יְדִיעַ לֶהֱוֵא לְמַלְכָּא.** דָּבָר יָדוּעַ יִהְיֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**Let it be known to the king** Let it be a known thing to the king.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:2)


**דִּי אֲזַלְנָא.** אֲשֶׁר הָלַכְנוּ אֶל אֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה הַמְּדִינָה לְבֵית אֱלוֹהַּ הַגָּדוֹל:


**that we went** that we went to the land of the province of Judea, to the House of the great God.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:3)


**וְהוּא מִתְבְּנֵא.** וְהוּא הַבַּיִת בָּנוּי:


**and it is built** And it, the House, is built.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:4)


**גָּלָל.** מַרְמְרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**marble** Aram. גְּלָּל (marmre in Old French), marble.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:5)


**וְאָע מִתְּשָׂם בְּכֻתְלַיָּא.** וְעֵצִים נְתוּנִים וּמְשׂוּמִים בַּכְּתָלִים כְּדֵי לְהַחֲזִיק הַבִּנְיָן לְמַעַן יַעֲמֹד יָמִים רַבִּים:


**and wood is laid in the walls** And wood is placed in the walls to reinforce the building in order that it endure for a long time.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:6)


**וַעֲבִידְתָא דָךְ.** וּמְלָאכָה זֹאת:


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:7)


**אָסְפַּרְנָא מִתְעַבְדָא.** מְהֵרָה הָיְתָה נַעֲשֵׂית:


**was done quickly** Aram. אָסְפַּרְנָא מִתְעַבְדָה, was done quickly.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:8)


**אָסְפַּרְנָא.** אֶפַנְטוֹמִינְשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


אָסְפַּרְנָא is (espleytosement in Old French), rapidly.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:8:9)


**וּמַצְלַח בִּידְהוֹם.** וְהֻצְלַח מַעֲשֶׂה זֶה בִּידֵיהֶם:


**and they succeeded at it** Aram. וּמַצְלַח בְּיֶדְהם. And this work was successful in their hands.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:1)


**אֱדַיִן.** אָז כְּשֶׁרָאִינוּ הַבִּנְיָן שָׁאַלְנוּ לַזְּקֵנִים הָאֵלֶּה:


**Then** When we saw the building, we asked these elders.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:2)


**כְּנֵמָא אֲמַרְנָא לְהוֹם.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר בְּסָמוּךְ אָמַרְנוּ אֲלֵיהֶם:


**as follows we said to them** As we will say immediately following, we said to them.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:3)


**מַן שָׂם לְכֹם.** מִי שָׂם לָכֶם דְּבַר הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה לִבְנוֹתוֹ, וּכְתָלִים הַלָּלוּ לִיסֹד?


**Who issued to you** Who issued to you the authorization to build this house and to found these walls?


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:9:4)


**וְאֻשַּׁרְנָא.** בִּנְיַן חוֹמַת לְבֵנִים:


**and...wall** the building of a wall of bricks.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:10:1)


**וְאַף שְׁמָהַתְּהוֹם.** וְאַף שְׁמוֹתָם שֶׁל בּוֹנִים רָאשֵׁי הַבִּנְיָן שָׁאַלְנוּ לָהֶם כְּדֵי לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ:


**also...their names** We also asked them the names of the builders, the heads of the construction, in order to let you know.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:10:2)


**דִּי נִכְתּוֹב.** אֲשֶׁר נִכְתּוֹב שְׁמוֹת הָאֲנָשִׁים אֵלֶיךָ:


**that we should write** that we should write to you the names of the men.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:10:3)


**דִּי בְרָאשֵׁיהֹם.** אֲשֶׁר בְּעַצְמָם וּבִרְשׁוּתָם קָמוּ לִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת:


**who are at their head** in whose counsel and authorization they arose to build the House.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:1)


**וּכְנֵמָא פִתְגָמָא.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר הַדָּבָר:


**And as follows** Aram. וּכְנֵמָא, as we shall state the matter.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:2)


**הֲתִיבוּנָא.** הָשִׁיבוּ לָנוּ דָּבָר:


**they gave us a reply** They answered us a word.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:3)


**אֲנַחְנָא הִמּוֹ.** אֲנַחְנוּ הֵם עַבְדָיו שֶׁל אֱלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ:


**We are** We are the servants of the God of the heavens and the earth.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:4)


**וּבִנְיַן בַּיְתָא.** וַאֲנַחְנוּ בּוֹנִין הַבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר הָיָה בָּנוּי קוֹדֶם לָכֵן שָׁנִים רַבּוֹת:


**and we are building the House** And we are building the House that was built many years ago.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:11:5)


**וּמֶלֶךְ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל רַב.** וּמֶלֶךְ גָּדוֹל שֶׁהָיָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא שְׁלֹמֹה הַמֶּלֶךְ בָּנָה אוֹתוֹ וְיִסַּד אוֹתוֹ:


**and a great king of Israel** And a great king that Israel had, viz. King Solomon, built it and founded it.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:12:1)


**לָהֵן.** וְעַכְשָׁיו מִפְּנֵי אֲשֶׁר הִכְעִיסוּ אֲבוֹתֵינוּ לֵאלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם מָסַר אוֹתָם בְּיַד מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל הַכַּשְׂדִּי וְהַבַּיִת הַזֶּה הֶחֱרִיב וְאֶת הָעָם הִגְלָה לְבָבֶל:


**But now** But now, since our forefathers angered the God of the heavens, He delivered them into the hands of the Chaldean king of Babylon, and he destroyed this House and exiled the people to Babylon.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:13:1)


**בְּרַם.** אַךְ בְּשָׁנָה אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל צִוָּה כוֹרֶשׁ בַּדָּבָר הַזֶּה לִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת:


**But** But in the first year of Cyrus, king of Babylon, Cyrus issued a command to build the House.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:1)


**וְאַף מָאנַיָּא.** כְּלֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ שֶׁל כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב אֲשֶׁר הוֹצִיא נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר מִן הַהֵיכָל שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם וְהוֹלִיכָם לְהֵיכָלוֹ שֶׁבְּבָבֶל, הוֹצִיא אוֹתָם כּוֹרֶשׁ מֵהֵיכַל מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל וּמְסָרָם לְיַד שֵׁשְׁבַּצַּר דָּנִיֵּאל שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ כָּךְ:


**And also the vessels** the holy vessels of silver and gold, which Nebuchadnezzar had taken out of the Temple that is in Jerusalem and brought to his temple that is in Babylon. Cyrus took them out of the temple of the king of Babylon and delivered them to the hand of Sheshbazzar—Daniel, who was called by that name.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:2)


**וְהֵיבִיל הִמּוֹ.** וְהוֹלִיךְ אוֹתָם:


**and brought them** [as translated,] and brought them.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:3)


**הַנְפֵּק הִמּוֹ.** הוֹצִיא אוֹתָם:


**had taken out** [as translated,] had taken them out.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:14:4)


**דִּי פֶחָה שְׁמֵהּ.** שֶׁהוּא הָיָה נָשִׂיא וּפַחַת, וְנִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ **פֶחָה**:


**whom he appointed governor** for he was a prince and a governor and was called by the name governor.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:15:1)


**וַאֲמַר לֵהּ.** וְכָךְ אָמַר לוֹ כּוֹרֶשׁ לְשֵׁשְׁבַּצַּר: אֵלֶּה הַכֵּלִים קַח וְלֵךְ וְהוֹרֵד אוֹתָם בַּהֵיכָל אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם וּבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא יְהֵא בָּנוּי עַל מְקוֹמוֹ:


**And he said to him** And so did Cyrus say to Sheshbazzar, “Take these vessels and bring them down into the Temple that is in Jerusalem, and the House of the Holy One, blessed be He, shall be built on its place.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:16:1)


**אֱדַיִן.** אָז:


**then** [as translated,] then.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:16:2)


**שֵׁשְׁבַּצַּר דָּךְ.** זֶה בָּא וְנָתַן כָּתְלֵי בֵּיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וּמֵאָז וְעַד עַתָּה הָיָה הַבַּיִת בָּנוּי וְלֹא נִגְמַר הַבִּנְיָן:


**this Sheshbazzar** Aram. דֵּךְ, came and built the walls of the House of the Holy One, blessed be He, that is in Jerusalem, and from then until now, the House has been built, but the building has not been completed.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezra 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:1)


**וּכְעַן.** וְעַתָּה אִם עַל הַמֶּלֶךְ טוֹב יִהְיֶה הַדָּבָר יָדוּעַ וּמְבֻקָּר בְּבֵית גְּנָזָיו שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ אֲשֶׁר שָׁם בְּבָבֶל:


**And now** And now, if it pleases the king, let the matter be known and searched out in the king’s archives, which is located in Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:2)


**הֵן אִיתַי.** אִם יֵשׁ הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר מִפִּי כּוֹרֶשׁ יָצָא דָּבָר לִבְנוֹת בַּיִת זֶה אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**whether it is so** whether this incident is so, that permission was issued by Cyrus to build this House, which is in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:3)


**וּרְעוּת מַלְכָּא.** רְצוֹן הַמֶּלֶךְ עַל כֵּן יִשְׁלַח אֵלֵינוּ:


**and the will of the king** And the will of the king concerning this he shall send to us.


###### Rashi on Ezra 5:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/5:17:4)


**עֲלֵינָא.** אֵלֵינוּ. עַד כָּאן מִכְתַּב הָאִגֶּרֶת אֲשֶׁר שָׁלְחוּ תַּתְּנַי וּשְׁתַר בּוֹזְנַי:


**to us** Aram. עֲלֶינָא, lit. on us. Until here is the text of the letter that Tattenai and Shethar-bozenai sent.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:1:1)


**בְּאֵדַיִן.** אָז כַּאֲשֶׁר הִגִּיעַ הַמִּכְתָּב לְיַד דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**Then** then when the letter reached Darius’s hand.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:1:2)


**שָׂם טְעֵם.** צִוָּה הַמֶּלֶךְ הַדָּבָר:


**gave an order** The king ordered the matter.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:1:3)


**וּבַקַּרוּ.** וּבָדְקוּ בְּבֵית הַסְּפָרִים שֶׁהָאוֹצָרוֹת מוּנָּחִים שָׁם בְּבָבֶל:


**and they searched** They searched in the library where the archives were stored in Babylon.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:2:1)


**וְהִשְׁתְּכַח בְּאַחְמְתָא.** וְנִמְצָא מִכְתָּב שֶׁל מְגִלָּה אַחַת שָׁם בְּאַחְמְתָא, כֵּן פָּתְרוּ אַנְשֵׁי הַפִּתְרוֹן שֶׁהוּא כְּלִי הֶעָשׂוּי לְאִגְּרוֹת וּלְגִלְיוֹנִים וְלִסְפָרִים, וְהֶעֱמִידוּהוּ מִגִּזְרַת ״חֵמֶת מַיִם״ (בראשית כא:יד), וְהָאָלֶ״ף אֲשֶׁר בַּתֵּיבָה אֵינָהּ עִקָּר כְּיֶתֶר הַמִּלִּין אֲשֶׁר בִּשְׁאָר לְשׁוֹן אֲרָמִית:


**And there was found in a pouch** And a text of one scroll was found there in an אַחְמְתָא. So did the interpreters interpret, that it is a receptacle made for letters, written sheets and books, and they established it from the root of (Gen. 21:14): “a skin of water (וְחֵמַת מַיִם),” and the “aleph” in the word is not a radical, like the rest of the Aramaic language.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:2:2)


**בְּבִירְתָא.** בְּאוֹתָהּ בִּירָה אֲשֶׁר בְּמָדַי הַמְּדִינָה:


**in the citadel** in that citadel that was in the province of Media.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:2:3)


**וְכֵן כְּתִיב.** וְכֵן הָיָה כָּתוּב בְּתוֹכָהּ זִכָּרוֹן שֶׁלָּהּ:


**and so was written** and so was written therein its memorandum.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:1)


**בֵּית אֱלָהָא בִירוּשְׁלֶם.** בְּבֵית אֱלֹהִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם מָה יְהֵא עָלָיו? הַבַּיִת יִבָּנֶה:


**the House of God in Jerusalem** regarding the House of God in Jerusalem; what should be with it? “The House should be built.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:2)


**אֲתַר דִּי דָבְחִין.** שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם שֶׁזּוֹבְחִים שָׁם זֶבַח:


**a place where they offer up sacrifices** which is a place where they offer up sacrifices.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:3)


**וְאֻשּׁוֹהִי מְסוֹבְלִין.** וְכוֹתְלָיו יִהְיוּ מְיֻסָּדִין, כְּמוֹ ״וְאֻשַּׁיָּא יַחִיטוּ״ (עזרא ד:יב):


**and its walls should be founded** Aram. וְאֻשּׁוֹהִי. And its walls should be founded, like (4:12): “and its walls (וְאֻשַּׁיָא) they have joined.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:3:4)


**רוּמֵיהּ אַמִּין שִׁתִּין.** רוּם הַבַּיִת שִׁשִּׁים אַמָּה וְשִׁשִּׁים אַמָּה רָחְבּוֹ:


**its height sixty cubits** the height of the House was sixty cubits, and sixty cubits was its width.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:4:1)


**נִדְבָּכִין.** לְשׁוֹן כְּתָלִים וְקִירוֹת הַכְּתָלִים הָיוּ שֶׁל אֶבֶן גָּלָל, גָּלָל מרמר״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**rows** Aram. נִדְבָּכִין, an expression for walls. The walls were of marble. **marble** Aram. גְּלָל (marmre in Old French).


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:4:2)


**וְנִדְבָּךְ.** וְכוֹתֶל שֶׁל עֵץ חָדָשׁ מִבִּפְנִים כְּעִנְיַן בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א ו:טו-יח):


**and a row** and a wall of new wood from within, in the manner of the building of the Temple that Solomon built.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:4:3)


**וְנִפְקְתָא.** וְהוֹצָאָה לְצֹרֶךְ הַבַּיִת וּלְשָׁאָר דְּבָרִים אֲשֶׁר לַבַּיִת תִּנָּתֵן לָהֶם מִבֵּית הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and the expenditures** And the expenditures for the needs of the House and for the other matters pertaining to the House shall be given them from the royal palace.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:1)


**וְאַף מָאנֵי.** וְאַף כְּלֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ שֶׁל בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים שֶׁל זָהָב וְכֶסֶף אֲשֶׁר נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר הוֹצִיאָם מִן הֵיכָל שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם וְהוֹלִיכָם לְבָבֶל:


**And also the vessels of** and also the sacred vessels of the House of God, of gold and silver, which Nebuchadnezzar took out of the Temple in Jerusalem and brought to Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:2)


**וְהֵיבִל.** אֲשֶׁר הוֹלִיךְ:


**let them be given over** [as translated,] let them be given over.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:3)


**יְהֲתִיבוּן.** יִנָּתְנוּ:


**and brought** which he brought.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:4)


**וְיִהַךְ.** יֵלֵךְ כָּל הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה לַהֵיכָל אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם לִמְקוֹמוֹ:


**and brought** Let all this work be brought to the Temple that is in Jerusalem, to its place.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:5:5)


**וְתַחַת.** וַיְהִי מוּנָּח בְּבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**and it shall be deposited** And it shall be deposited in the House of the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:6:1)


**כְּעַן תַּתְּנַי וְגוֹ׳.** זוֹ הִיא תְּשׁוּבָה אֲשֶׁר הֵשִׁיב דָּרְיָוֶשׁ אֶל תַּתְּנַי וּשְׁתַר בּוֹזְנַי:


**Now, Tattenai** This is the reply with which Darius answered Tattenai and Shethar-Bozenai.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:6:2)


**דִּי בַּעֲבַר נַהֲרָה.** לְצַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְאוֹתָן שֶׁבְּבָבֶל:


**who are on the other side of the river** to the side of the land of Israel, which is the other side of the river to those in Babylon


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:6:3)


**רְחִיקִין הֲווֹ מִן תַּמָּה.** רְחוֹקִים תִּהְיוּ מִשָּׁם, מִבִּנְיַן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁלֹּא תַּרְפּוּ יְדֵי עוֹשֵׂי הַמְּלָאכָה:


**distance yourselves from there** Distance yourselves from the building of the Temple, so that you do not disturb the workers.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:1)


**דַּךְ.** זֶה:


**this** [as translated,] this.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:2)


**פַּחַת יְהוּדָיֵא.** שָׁלְטוֹנֵי הַיְּהוּדִים יַעֲשׂוּ דָּבָר זֶה:


**the governors of the Jews** The rulers of the Jews shall do this thing.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:3)


**וּלְשָׂבֵי יְהוּדָיֵא.** וְלִזְקְנֵי הַיְּהוּדִים אֲנִי מְצַוֶּה:


**and to the elders of the Jews** And to the elders of the Jews I command.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:7:4)


**בֵּית אֱלָהָא דֵךְ.** בֵּית אֱלֹהִים זֶה:


**this House of God** [as translated,] this House of God.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:1)


**וּמִנִּי שִׂים טְעֵם.** וּמִמֶּנִּי יִהְיֶה צִוּוּי זֶה:


**And from me shall be issued an order** And from me this command will go forth.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:2)


**לְמָא דִי תַעַבְדוּן.** לְאוֹתוֹ בִּנְיַן הַמְּלָאכָה אֲשֶׁר תַּעֲשׂוּן עִם זִקְנֵי הַיְּהוּדִים הָאֵלֶּה לִבְנוֹת בֵּיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**regarding what you shall do** regarding that building, the work that you shall do with these elders of the Jews to build the House of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:3)


**וּמִנִּכְסֵי מַלְכָּא.** מִנִּכְסֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ יִהְיוּ עוֹשִׂים הַבִּנְיָן הַזֶּה:


**and from the king’s property** And from the king’s property they shall make ths building.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:4)


**דִּי מִדַּת עֲבַר נַהֲרָה.** מִן הַמַּס שֶׁל עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר אֲשֶׁר לַמֶּלֶךְ, מִדַּת מַס כְּמוֹ ״מִנְדָּה בְלוֹ וַהֲלָךְ״ (עזרא ד:יג). **אָסְפַּרְנָא.** מְהֵרָה יִבָּנֶה הַבִּנְיָן. אָסְפַּרְנָה אישפלונטמנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**from the taxes of the other side of the river** from the taxes of the other side of the river, which belong to the king. **taxes** Aram. מִדַּת, tax, like (4:13): “the king’s due (מִנְדָּה), the head tax, or the meal tax.” **quickly** Aram. אָסְפַּרְנָא. The building shall quickly be built; esploytosemant in Old French.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:8:5)


**נִפְקְתָא תְּהֵא מִתְיַהֲבָא.** הוֹצָאָה תִּהְיֶה נִתֶּנֶת לָאֲנָשִׁים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִתְבַּטְּלוּ מִמְּלַאכְתָּן:


**The expenses shall be given** The expenses shall be given to these men so that they should not idle from their work.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:1)


**וּמָה חַשְׁחָן.** וּמָה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ חוֹשְׁשִׁין:


**And what they require** and what they are concerned about.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:2)


**וּבְנֵי תוֹרִין.** בְּנֵי בָקָר וְאֵילִים וּכְבָשִׂים לְעוֹלוֹת לֵאלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמָיִם:


**and young bulls** young bulls, rams and lambs for burnt offerings to the God of heaven.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:3)


**וְחִנְטִין.** לְסֹלָתוֹת וּמֶלַח לְקָרְבָּנוֹת. **וְיַיִן** לִנְסָכִים. **וְשֶׁמֶן** לִמְנָחוֹת, כַּאֲשֶׁר יֹאמְרוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם יְהִי נָתוּן לָהֶם דְּבַר יוֹם בְּיוֹמוֹ:


**wheat** for fine flour. **salt** for sacrifices. **wine** for libations. **and oil** for the meal-offerings. When the priests who are in Jerusalem will say [what they need], that shall be given to them the requirements of each day on its day.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:9:4)


**דִּי לָא שָׁלוּ.** בְּלֹא דִּיחוּי, שנ״ץ דישטורבי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**without delay** Aram. דִּי לָא שָּׁלוּ (son destorber in Old French).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:10:1)


**דִּי לֶהֱוֹן.** לְמַעַן אֲשֶׁר יִהְיוּ מַקְרִיבִים נִיחוֹחִים לֵאלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם וְיִהְיוּ מִתְפַּלְּלִים עַל חַיֵּי הַמֶּלֶךְ וּבָנָיו:


**That they should** in order that they should offer up pleasing sacrificing to the God of the heavens, and that they should pray for the life of the king and his children.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:1)


**וּמִנִּי שִׂים טְעֵם.** וּמִמֶּנִּי שִׂים צִוּוּי וְשִׂימַת דָּבָר אֲשֶׁר כָּל אָדָם אֲשֶׁר יְשַׁנֶּה דָּבָר זֶה


**And an order is issued by me** And a command is issued by me, along with the issue of a word regarding any person who disobeys this word.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:2)


**יִתְנְסַח.** יֵחָרֵב עֵצָיו מִבֵּיתוֹ שֶׁיֵּחָרֵב בֵּיתוֹ, יִתְנְסַח כְּמוֹ ״וְנִסַּחְתֶּם מֵעַל הָאֲדָמָה״ (דברים כח:סג):


**shall be torn** Aram. יִתְנְסַח. The beams of his house shall be destroyed. יִתְנְסַח is like (Deut. 28:63): “and you shall be uprooted (וְנִסַחְתֶּם) from the land.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:3)


**וּזְקִיף יִתְמְחֵא עֲלוֹהִי.** וְעֵץ לִתְלוֹתוֹ יְהֵא גָבוֹהַּ עָלָיו שֶׁיִּתָּלֶה עַל הָעֵץ:


**and a gallows upon which to place him** And a gallows upon which to hang him shall be [raised up] high, so that he be hanged upon the gallows.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:4)


**וּבַיְתֵהּ נְוָלוּ יִתְעֲבֵד.** וּבֵיתוֹ יֵעָשֶׂה אַשְׁפָּה עַל כָּךְ:


**and his house shall be made a dungheap** and his house shall be made a dungheap because of this.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:11:5)


**נְוָלוּ.** עִנְיַן טִנּוּף וּמַחֲרָאוֹת:


נְוָלוּ connotes filth and excrement.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:12:1)


**וֵאלָהָא דִּי שַׁכִּן שְׁמֵיהּ.** וְהָאֱלֹהִים אֲשֶׁר שִׁכֵּן שְׁמוֹ שָׁם:


**And God, Who caused His name to rest there** [as translated,] and God, Who caused His name to rest there.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:12:2)


**יְמַגַּר.** יְמַגֵּר וִיפַגֵּר כָּל אוֹתוֹ מֶלֶךְ וְכָל אוֹתוֹ עַם אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁלַח יָדוֹ לְהַחֲרִיב וּלְהַשְׁחִית בֵּיתוֹ זֶה שֶׁל ה׳ אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלָיִם:


**will cast down** will cast down and destroy any such king and any such people that will stretch forth a hand to lay waste and destroy His house that is in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:12:3)


**שַׂמְתּ טְעֵם.** שַׂמְתִּי צִוּוּי דָּבָר זֶה אֲשֶׁר יַעֲשֶׂה הַבִּנְיָן מְהֵרָה. עַד כָּאן תְּשׁוּבָתוֹ שֶׁל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**have issued an edict** I have issued a command concerning this matter, that the building be constructed swiftly. Until here is Darius’s reply.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:13:1)


**כְּנֵמָא.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר:


**as was said** [as translated,] as was said.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:13:2)


**אָסְפַּרְנָא עֲבַדוּ.** מְהֵרָה עֲשׂוּ׃


**acted swiftly** [as translated,] acted swiftly.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:14:1)


**וְשָׂבֵי יְהוּדָיֵא.** וְזִקְנֵי הַיְּהוּדִים הָיוּ בּוֹנִין וּמַצְלִיחִין בִּמְלַאכְתָּן כִּנְבוּאַת חַגַּי וּזְכַרְיָה, וּבָנוּ וְיִסְּדוּ אֶת הַבִּנְיָן מִן צִוּוּי שֶׁל אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמֵרְשׁוּת כּוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס הָרִאשׁוֹן אֲשֶׁר הוּחַל יְסוֹד הַבִּנְיָן בְּיָמָיו, וּמֵרְשׁוּת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ זֶה מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס:


**And the elders of the Jews** And the elders of the Jews were building and succeeding in their work, according to the prophecy of Haggai and Zechariah, and they built and founded the building by the command of the God of Israel and by the authorization of Cyrus, the first king of Persia, in whose days the laying of the foundation was commenced, and by the authorization of this Darius, the king of Persia.


###### Rashi on Ezra 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:14:2)


**וְאַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתְּא.** הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ וְנִקְרָא אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתְּא עַל שֵׁם הַמְּדִינָה וְהַמַּלְכוּת, כִּי כָל מַלְכֵי פָרַס הָיוּ נִקְרָאִים כָּךְ, כַּאֲשֶׁר כָּל מַלְכֵי מִצְרַיִם נִקְרָאִים פַּרְעֹה:


**and Artaxerxes** He is Darius, but he was called Artaxerxes because of the province and the kingdom, for all kings of Persia were thus named just as all the kings of Egypt were called Pharaoh.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:15:1)


**וְשֵׁיצִיאָ בַּיְתָה דְנָה.** וְסוֹף תַּשְׁלוּם בַּיִת זֶה הָיָה עַד יוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי לְחֹדֶשׁ **אֲדָר** שֶׁהִיא שְׁנַת שֵׁשׁ לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְנִמְצָא בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת הָיָה אַרְבַּע שָׁנִים:


**And the completion of this House** And the end of the completion of this House was until the third day of the month of Adar, which is the sixth year of King Darius; hence the building of the House took four years.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:17:1)


**וְהִקְרִיבוּ לַחֲנֻכַּת.** הִקְרִיבוּ לַחֲנֻכַּת בַּיִת זֶה קָרְבָּנוֹת הַלָּלוּ, וְהוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה הָיְתָה:


**And they offered up for the dedication** They offered up for the dedication of this House these sacrifices, and it was a temporary ruling.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:18:1)


**וַהֲקִימוּ כָהֲנַיָּא בִּפְלֻגָּתְהוֹן.** שֶׁהָיוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים חֲלוּקִים בַּעֲבוֹדָתָם וְלֵוִיִּם בְּמִשְׁמְרוֹת:


**Now they set up the priests in their divisions** for the priests were divided in their service and the Levites in their watches.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:19:1)


**אֶת הַפֶּסַח.** עַכְשָׁיו עָשׂוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַפֶּסַח בְּאַרְבַּעָה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן זֶה הַבָּא אַחַר אֲדָר זֶה שֶׁהֻשְׁלַם הַבִּנְיָן בּוֹ:


**the Passover [offering]** Now Israel made the Passover offering on this fourteenth of Nissan, which followed this Adar, when the building was completed.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:20:1)


**וְלָהֶם.** וּלְעַצְמָם:


**and for themselves** Heb. וְלָהֶם, lit. and for them, and for themselves.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:21:1)


**וְכֹל הַנִּבְדָּל.** הֵם הַגֵּרִים שֶׁנִּבְדְּלוּ מִטֻּמְאַת הָעֲבוֹדָה כּוֹכָבִים לִידָּבֵק בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and whoever separated** They are the proselytes, who were separated from the defilement of the nations to cleave to Israel.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezra 6:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/6:22:1)


**וְהֵסֵב לֵב מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר עֲלֵיהֶם.** וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא הֵסֵב לֵב מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּמֶמְשַׁלְתּוֹ בְּאֶרֶץ אַשּׁוּר לְהַחֲזִיקָם וּלְסַיֵּעַ בִּמְלֶאכֶת הַבִּנְיָן:


**and turned the heart of the king of Assyria toward them** And the Holy One, blessed be He, turned the heart of the king of Assyria toward the Israelites who were dwelling under his dominion in the land of Assyria, to strengthen them and to assist in the work of the building.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:1:1)


**וְאַחַר הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה.** שֶׁל בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת:


**And after these incidents** of the building of the House.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:1:2)


**אַרְתַּחְשַׁסְתְּא.** הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**Artaxerxes** That is Darius.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:6:1)


**וַיִּתֶּן לוֹ הַמֶּלֶךְ.** דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**and the king granted him** [i.e., King] Darius.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:6:2)


**כְּיַד ה׳ אֱלֹהָיו עָלָיו.** כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוָּה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עָלָיו, וּמִקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא: **וַיִּתֶּן לוֹ הַמֶּלֶךְ כָּל בַּקָּשָׁתוֹ כְּיַד ה׳ אֱלֹהָיו עָלָיו.** וְאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה עֶזְרָא הוּא מַלְאָכִי (מגילה טו.):


**according to the command of the Lord his God upon him** as the Holy One, blessed be He, had commanded him. This verse is transposed [its meaning being]: and the King granted him his entire request, according to the command of the Lord upon him. And our Rabbis said (Meg. 15a) that Ezra and Malachi were one and the same.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:7:1)


**בִּשְׁנַת שֶׁבַע.** הִיא שָׁנָה אַחַר תַּשְׁלוּם הַבַּיִת:


**in the seventh year** That is a year after the completion of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:7:2)


**לְאַרְתַּחְשַׁסְתְּא.** הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**of Artaxerxes** That is Darius.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:8:1)


**בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַחֲמִישִׁי.** הוּא חֹדֶשׁ אָב, כִּי בְּאֶחָד לַחֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם הָיְתָה תְּחִלַּת עֲלִיָּתָם מִבָּבֶל, וּבְאֶחָד לְחֹדֶשׁ אָב בָּאוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**in the fifth month** i.e., the month of Av, for the first of the month of Nissan was the beginning of their ascent from Babylon, and on the first of the month of Av they arrived in Jerusalem.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:9:1)


**יְסוֹד הַמַּעֲלָה.** יְסוֹד תְּחִלַּת הָעֲלִיָּה:


**was the commencement of the ascent** the foundation of the commencement of the ascent.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:9:2)


**כְּיַד אֱלֹהָיו.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הִצְלִיחוֹ יַד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהָיְתָה יָדוֹ **טוֹבָה עָלָיו** לְהַצְלִיחוֹ בִּדְרָכָיו:


**according to the...hand of his God** as the hand of the Holy One, blessed be He, caused him to succeed, for His hand was good upon him to make him succeed in his way.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:10:1)


**כִּי עֶזְרָא וְגוֹ׳.** וְעַל כֵּן הָלַךְ וְהִצְלִיחַ בְּדַרְכּוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי **עֶזְרָא הֵכִין לְבָבוֹ וְגוֹמֵר,** נוֹתֵן טַעַם לִדְבָרָיו:


**For Ezra...** Therefore, he went and prospered in his way, because Ezra prepared his heart, etc. He gives a reason for his words.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:11:1)


**וְזֶה פַּרְשֶׁגֶן הַנִּשְׁתְּוָן.** וְזֶה פַּתְשֶׁגֶן הַכְּתָב:


**And this is the interpretation of the writ** Heb. פַתְשֶּׁגֶן הַכְּתָב.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:11:2)


**וְחֻקָּיו עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** וְאֶת חֻקָּיו אֲשֶׁר צִוָּה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל לְשָׁמְרָם:


**and His statutes to Israel** and His statutes that He commanded Israel to keep.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:12:1)


**סָפַר דָּתָא.** סֵפֶר תּוֹרָה:


**the Book of Law** [as translated,] the Book of Law.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:12:2)


**גְּמִיר וּכְעֶנֶת.** סְפֵיקָא הִיא בְּיָדוֹ:


**who has mastered... and Ke’eneth** He has mastered it in his hand.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:13:1)


**מִנִּי שִׂים טְעֵם.** מִמֶּנִּי יוֹצֵא הָרְשׁוּת כִּי כָּל אֲשֶׁר נָדְבָה רוּחוֹ אוֹתוֹ מִן יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם לָלֶכֶת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם יֵלְכוּ עִמָּךְ:


**An order is issued by me** The authorization emanates from me that whoever of Israel, of the priests, or of the Levites, whose heart prompts him to go to Jerusalem, may go with you.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:14:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל.** בִּשְׁבִיל כָּךְ מֵאֵת הַמֶּלֶךְ וְשִׁבְעַת יוֹעֲצָיו רוֹאֵי פְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ אַתָּה שָׁלוּחַ:


**Because of this** Because of this, you are sent from the king and his seven advisors who saw the king’s face.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:14:2)


**וְשִׁבְעַת יָעֲטוֹהִי.** וְשֶׁבַע יוֹעֲצָיו הֵם שִׁבְעָה רוֹאֵי פְּנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ (אסתר א:יד):


**and his seven advisors** and his seven advisors; they are the seven who see the king’s face.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:14:3)


**לְבַקָּרָה עַל יְהוּד.** כְּדֵי לִדְרֹשׁ וְלִבְדֹּק עַל הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם אִם הֵם עֲסוּקִים בְּתוֹרָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר בְּיָדְךָ:


**to search out Judea** An order to seek and to search out the Jews who are in Jerusalem, to see whether they are engaged in the Torah of the Holy One, blessed be He, which is in your hand.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:15:1)


**וּלְהֵיבָלָה.** וּלְהוֹלִיךְ כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב שֶׁל הַמֶּלֶךְ וְיוֹעֲצָיו אֲשֶׁר הִתְנַדֵּב לֵאלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְתַקֵּן בֵּיתוֹ אֲשֶׁר מִשְׁכָּנוֹ וּבֵיתוֹ בִּירוּשָׁלָיִם:


**And to bring** and to bring the silver and gold of the king and his advisors that they donated to the God of Israel, to maintain His House, Whose habitation and House are in Jerusalem.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:16:1)


**וְכָל כֶּסֶף וּדְהַב.** וְכָל נִדְבַת כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב אֲשֶׁר תִּמְצָא בִּמְדִינַת בָּבֶל עִם שְׁאָר נִדְבַת הָעָם וְהַכֹּהֲנִים אֲשֶׁר מִתְנַדְּבִים לְבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, תּוֹלִיךְ הַכֹּל לִירוּשָׁלָיִם:


**And all the silver and gold** and all the silver and gold that you will find in the province of Babylon, together with the donations of the people and the priests that they will donate to the House of the Holy One, blessed be He, which is in Jerusalem; you shall take everything to Jerusalem.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:17:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דְּנָה.** וּבִשְׁבִיל כָּךְ שֶׁל עֲבוֹדוֹת הַמִּקְדָּשׁ מְהֵרָה תִּקְנֶה בְּכֶסֶף זֶה בָּקָר וְאֵילִים וּכְבָשִׂים וּזְבָחִים וּמְנָחוֹת וּנְסָכִים, וְתַקְרִיבֵם עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Because of this** And because of this, because it is for the service of the Sanctuary, you shall quickly purchase with this money cattle, rams and lambs; sacrifices, meal-offerings, and libations, and you shall offer them up on the altar of the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:18:1)


**וּמָה דִי עֲלָךְ.** וַאֲשֶׁר יִיטַב לְךָ וּלְאַחֶיךָ בְּמוֹתַר הַכֶּסֶף וְהַזָּהָב לַעֲשׂוֹת כִּרְצוֹנוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא תַּעֲשׂוּ:


**And what pleases you** And what pleases you and your brethren (to do) with the rest of the silver and the gold, according to the will of the Holy One, blessed be He, you shall do.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:19:1)


**וּמָאנַיָּא.** וְכֵלִים הַנְּתוּנִים לְךָ לַעֲבוֹדָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שְׁלֵם לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**And the vessels** And the vessels that are given to you for the service of the Holy One, blessed be He, give in their entirety before the Holy One, blessed be He, in Jerusalem.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:20:1)


**וּשְׁאָר חַשְׁחוּת.** וּשְׁאָר צְרִיכוּת שֶׁתִּצְטָרֵךְ לְבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִקְנוֹת, אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה לְךָ לִיתֵּן הַדָּמִים תִּתֵּן מֵאוֹצַר הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**And the rest of the necessities** and the rest of the necessities that you will require for the House of the Holy One, blessed be He, to purchase, for which you will have to give the money; you shall give from the royal treasury.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:21:1)


**וּמִנִּי אֲנָה.** וּמִמֶּנִּי אֲנִי דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ יוֹצֵא צִוּוּי וְרָשׁוּת לְכָל הַגִּזְבָּרִים אֲשֶׁר בְּעֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְצַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הַמְקַבְּלִים מִסִּים שֶׁלִּי מִן הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים אֲשֶׁר כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁיִּשְׁאַל מִכֶּם עֶזְרָא הַכֹּהֵן סוֹפֵר הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְהֵרָה יֵעָשֶׂה שֶׁלֹּא יְעַכְּבוּ אֶת שְׁאֵלָתוֹ עַד מֵאָה כִּכָּרִים שֶׁל כֶּסֶף לִקְנוֹת קָרְבָּנוֹת וּמֵאָה כוֹרִין חִטִּין לִמְנָחוֹת וְעַד מֵאָה מִדּוֹת יַיִן לִנְסָכִים וּמֵאָה (מִדּוֹת) שֶׁל שֶׁמֶן לִבְלוֹל מְנָחוֹת וּמֶלַח לְקָרְבָּנוֹת כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְכָל קָרְבַּן מִנְחָתְךָ בַּמֶּלַח תִּמְלָח״ (ויקרא ב:יג):


**And from me—I** And from me—I, King Darius—is issued a command and an authorization to all the treasurers who are on the other side of the river of the side of Israel, who receive my taxes from the nations, that anything that Ezra the priest, the scholar of the Law of the Holy One, blessed be He, requests of you, shall be performed quickly. They should not delay his request up to a hundred talents of silver, with which to purchase sacrifices, and a hundred kors of wheat for meal-offerings, and up to a hundred measures of wine for the libations, and a hundred measures of oil to mingle the meal- offerings, and salt for the sacrifices, according to the matter that is stated (Lev. 2:13): “And every meal-offering you shall salt with salt.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:22:1)


**דִּי לָא כְתַב.** וּמֶלַח שֶׁאֵין כָּתוּב בּוֹ מִדָּה יִתְּנוּ כְּכָל הַצֹּרֶךְ:


**which is not written** And salt, regarding which no measure is written, they shall give according to how much is needed.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:23:1)


**כָּל דִּי מִן טַעַם.** כָּל אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה מֵרְשׁוּתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְצִוּוּיוֹ יֵעָשֶׂה בִּזְרִיזוּת בְּחוֹתַם הַמֶּלֶךְ לְבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Whatever is with the authorization** Whatever is with the authorization of the Holy One, blessed be He, and His command, shall be done with alacrity, with the seal of the king for the House of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:23:2)


**אַדְרַזְדָּא.** חוֹתָם בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי:


**with a seal** Aram. אַדְרַזְדָּא, seal in Arabic.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:23:3)


**דִּי לְמָה לֶהֱוֵא.** אֲשֶׁר לָמָּה יִהְיֶה קֶצֶף עַל מַלְכוּת הַמֶּלֶךְ וּבָנָיו, וְעַל כֵּן יִתְּנוּ הַגִּזְבָּרִים כָּל צָרְכֵי הַבַּיִת:


**for why should there be** For why should there be wrath against the realm of the king and his sons? Therefore, the treasurers shall provide all the needs of the House.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:24:1)


**וּלְכוֹם מְהוֹדְעִין**. וּלְכֶם אָנוּ מוֹדִיעִים אֲשֶׁר כָּל הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים וְהַשּׁוֹעֲרִים וְהַנְּתִינִים שֶׁהֵם חוֹטְבֵי עֵצִים וְשׁוֹאֲבֵי מַיִם לַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְעוֹבְדִים בְּבַיִת זֶה שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לֹא יִשְׁלוֹט אָדָם עֲלֵיהֶם לְהַשְׁלִיךְ עֲלֵיהֶם מַס וְכֶסֶף גֻּלְגֹּלֶת, אֶלָּא יִהְיוּ בְּנֵי חוֹרִין מֵעֲבוֹדַת הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**And we are making known to you** And we are making known to you that [over] all the priests, the Levites, the musicians, the gatekeepers, and the Nethinites, who are wood cutters and water drawers for the altar, and those who serve in this House of the Holy One, no man shall be empowered over them to levy upon them any tribute or head tax, but they shall be free from the service of the king.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:25:1)


**וְאַנְתְּ עֶזְרָא כְּחָכְמַת אֱלָהָךְ דִּי בִידָךְ.** וְאַתָּה עֶזְרָא כְּפִי חָכְמַת אֱלֹהֶיךָ אֲשֶׁר בְּיָדֶיךָ יוֹתֵר מִן הַשּׁוֹפְטִים וְהַדַּיָּנִים שֶׁהֵם דָּנִין כָּל הָעָם שֶׁבְּעֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְצַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל עִם יוֹדְעֵי הַתּוֹרָה:


**And you, Ezra, according to the wisdom of your God, which is in your hand** and you, Ezra, according to the wisdom of your God, which is in your hand [and which is] greater than that of the judges and the magistrates who judge all the people beyond the river to the side of Israel, the people knowledgeable in the Torah.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:25:2)


**וְדִי לָא יָדַע.** וְהַשּׁוֹפֵט אֲשֶׁר לֹא יָדַע לִשְׁפּוֹט, תּוֹדִיעַ לוֹ הַמִּשְׁפָּט לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ:


**and whoever does not know** and the judge who does not know how to judge, inform him of the judgment that he should execute it.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:1)


**וְכָל דִּי לָא לֶהֱוֵא עָבֵד.** וְכָל אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִהְיֶה עוֹשֶׂה דָּתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְדַת הַמֶּלֶךְ, מְהֵרָה יְעֻכַּב הַדִּין וְהַמִּשְׁפָּט:


**And whoever does not fulfill** and whoever does not fulfill the law of the Holy One, blessed be He, and the law of the king promptly, viz. that he delays the law and the judgment.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:2)


**דִּינָה.** הַדִּין יֵעָשֶׂה מִמֶּנּוּ מִן הַמְעַכֵּב מִשְׁפָּט:


**judgment** The judgment shall be inflicted upon him, upon the one who obstructs justice.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:3)


**הֵן לָמוּת.** לַהֲרֹג:


**whether to be executed** [as translated,] whether to be executed.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:4)


**הֵן לִשְׁרוֹשִׁי.** אִם לְעָקְרוֹ מִן הָעוֹלָם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת זַרְעוֹ וְאֶת מִשְׁפַּחְתּוֹ, לִשְׁרוֹשִׁי דישראצינ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**uprooted** whether to uproot him from the world, him, his offspring and his family (לִשְּׁרשִׁי is desraziner in Old French, to uproot).


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:5)


**הֵן לַעֲנוֹשׁ נִכְסִין.** אִם לְעָנְשׁוֹ בְּמָמוֹן:


**fined** to be punished with money.


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:26:6)


**וְלֶאֱסוּרִין.** אוֹ לְיַסְּרוֹ בְּיִסּוּרִין. עַד כָּאן מִכְתַּב הָאִגֶּרֶת אֲשֶׁר נָתַן דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ לְעֶזְרָא הַסּוֹפֵר לְמַעַן יִשָּׂאֵהוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם לְהַרְאוֹתוֹ אֶל הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם וְגִזְבָּרֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ, לְמַעַן יַחֲזִיקוּ לְקַיֵּם הַמִּצְוֹת וְלַעֲבֹד ה׳ בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וְלִשְׁמֹר הַתּוֹרָה כַּמִּשְׁפָּט:


**or tortured** or to torture him with tortures. Until here is the writ of the letter that King Darius gave to Ezra the scribe in order that he convey it to Jerusalem to show it to the priests and the Levites and the king’s treasurers, in order that they persist in performing the commandments and worshiping the Lord in the Temple and keeping the Torah properly.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:27:1)


**בָּרוּךְ ה׳.** כָּךְ כָּתַב עֶזְרָא בְּסִפְרוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר נָתַן הוֹדָאָה לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Blessed be the Lord** Ezra wrote this in his Book when he gave thanks to the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezra 7:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:28:1)


**וְיוֹעֲצָיו.** הֵם שִׁבְעָה רוֹאֵי פְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ, הֵן שִׁבְעָה יְעָטוּהִי שֶׁל מַעְלָה:


**and his advisers** They are his seven advisers mentioned above (verse 14).


###### Rashi on Ezra 7:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/7:28:2)


**כְּיַד ה׳ אֱלֹהַי עָלָי.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הָיְתָה עֶזְרָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עָלַי, וְקִבַּצְתִּי מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל הַנִּשְׁאָרִים בְּבָבֶל רָאשֵׁי אָבוֹת וּגְדוֹלִים לַעֲלוֹת עִמִּי מִבָּבֶל:


**according to the hand of the Lord my God upon me** as the help of the Holy One, blessed be He, was upon me; and I gathered from the Israelites remaining in Babylon the chiefs of the fathers’ houses and the great men to ascend with me from Babylon.



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:1:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה רָאשֵׁי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם.** שֶׁל עוֹלִים:


**And these are the heads of their fathers’ houses** of the immigrants.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:1:2)


**וְהִתְיַחְשָׂם.** אֶת יִחוּסָם אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ מִתְיַחֲשִׂים בָּהֶם:


**and the lineage** and their lineage to which they attributed their descent.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:1:3)


**אַרְתַּחְשַׁסְתְּא.** הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**Artaxerxes** He is Darius.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:3:1)


**מִבְּנֵי שְׁכַנְיָה מִבְּנֵי פַרְעֹשׁ.** מִשְׁפָּחָה אַחַת הִיא, וְזְכַרְיָה הוּא רֹאשׁ הַמִּשְׁפָּחָה:


**Of the sons of Shechaniah, of the sons of Parosh** That is one family, and Zechariah was the head of the family.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:3:2)


**וְעִמּוֹ.** וְעִם יַחַס שֶׁלּוֹ הָיוּ מִתְיַחֲסִים הַזְּכָרִים שֶׁל מִשְׁפַּחְתּוֹ:


**and with him** With his lineage, the males of his family traced their lineage.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:6:1)


**עֶבֶד בֶּן יוֹנָתָן.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ:


**Ebed the son of Jonathan** That was his name.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:13:1)


**אַחֲרוֹנִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ אַחֲרוֹנִים לָלֶכֶת אַחֲרֵי עֶזְרָא:


**the last ones** who were the last ones to go after Ezra.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:1)


**אֶל הַנָּהָר הַבָּא.** אֶל הַנָּהָר הַנּוֹבֵעַ:


**to the flowing river** Heb. אֶל הַנָהָר הַבָּא, lit. to the coming river.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:2)


**אֶל אַהֲוָא.** אֶל אוֹתוֹ הַנָּהָר:


**to Ahava** to that river.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:3)


**וְנַחֲנֶה שָׁם.** וְהָיִינוּ חוֹנִים שָׁם:


**and we encamped there** and we were camping there.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:15:4)


**וָאָבִינָה בָעָם.** נָתַתִּי לִבִּי לְהָבִין בָּעָם אֲשֶׁר נִקְבְּצוּ עִמִּי:


**and I surveyed the people** I directed my attention to survey the people who had gathered with me.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:16:1)


**וָאֶשְׁלְחָה לֶאֱלִיעֶזֶר וְגוֹ׳.** רָאשִׁים, גְּדוֹלִים:


**And I sent to Eliezer...the heads** the great men.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:16:2)


**מְבִינִים.** חֲכָמִים הָיוּ:


**the wise men** Heb. מְבִינִים, lit. understanding. They were wise men.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:1)


**וָאֲצַוֶּה אוֹתָם וְגוֹ׳.** שְׁלַחְתִּים וְצִוִּיתִים לְדַבֵּר אֶל **אִדּוֹ** שֶׁהוּא גָּדוֹל וְחָשׁוּב אֲשֶׁר **בְּכָסִפְיָא**:


**And I commanded them, etc.** I sent them and commanded them to speak to Iddo, who was the greatest and most esteemed in Casiphia.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:2)


**עַל אֲדוֹ.** אֶל **אֲדוֹ**:


**to Iddo** Heb. עַל אִדוֹ, lit. about Iddo.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:3)


**וָאָשִׂימָה בְּפִיהֶם.** שַׂמְתִּי בְּפִיהֶם דְּבָרִים לְדַבֵּר בִּשְׁלִיחוּתִי אֶל אִדּוֹ וְאֶחָיו שֶׁהֵם נְתוּנִים וּמְיֻשָּׁבִים בְּכָסִפְיָא לְהָבִיא לָנוּ מְשָׁרְתִים לַעֲבֹד בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and I put...into their mouth** I put into their mouth words to speak as my emissary to Iddo and his brother, who were placed and settled in Casiphia, to bring us servants to serve in the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:4)


**בְּכָסִפְיָא.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם בְּבָבֶל:


**in Casiphia** the name of a place in Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:5)


**אֶל אִדּוֹ אָחִיו.** שֵׁם אָדָם:


**Ahiv** the name of a man.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:17:6)


**הַנְּתוּנִים.** כְּמוֹ הַנְּתִינִים, וְעַל כֵּן נִכְתַּב בְּוָי״ו:


**who were settled** Heb. הַנְתִינִים, like הַנְתוּנִים, those who were settled. Therefore, it is written with a “vav.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:18:1)


**כְּיַד אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הָיְתָה הַצְלָחָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עָלֵינוּ:


**according to the hand of our God** just as the success of the Holy One, blessed be He, was upon us.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:18:2)


**אִישׁ שֶׂכֶל.** חָכָם:


**a man of intellect** a wise man.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:18:3)


**מִבְּנֵי מַחְלִי וְגוֹ׳.** כֻּלָּם הַלָּלוּ לְוִיִּם:


**of the sons of Mahli...** All these were Levites.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:20:1)


**וּמִן הַנְּתִינִים.** וּמִן הָעֲבָדִים נְתִינִים שֶׁנָּתַן דָּוִד הַמֶּלֶךְ וְשָׂרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עָלוּ עִמָּהֶם:


**And of the Nethinites** And of the slaves, the Nethnites that King David had appointed, and the chefs of Israel ascended with them.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:21:1)


**לְהִתְעַנּוֹת.** לֵישֵׁב בְּתַעֲנִית:


**to fast** Heb. לְהִתְעַנוֹת to fast.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:21:2)


**דֶּרֶךְ יְשָׁרָה.** שֶׁיּוֹלִיכֵנִי הַמָּקוֹם לְשָׁלוֹם:


**a straight way** that the Omnipresent lead us in peace.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:22:1)


**כִּי בֹשְׁתִּי.** שֶׁהֲרֵי נִתְבַּיַּשְׁתִּי **מִן הַמֶּלֶךְ** שֶׁלֹּא רָצִיתִי **לִשְׁאֹל** לוֹ רֶכֶב **וּפָרָשִׁים** כְּדֵי לְתַיְּרֵנוּ:


**For I was ashamed** For I was ashamed of the king, and I did not wish to request of him chariots and horsemen in order to guide us.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:22:2)


**כִּי אָמַרְנוּ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּבָר אָמַרְנוּ אֶל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵטִיב לִמְבַקְשָׁיו וְשׁוֹפֵךְ אַפּוֹ וַחֲמָתוֹ עַל עוֹזְבָיו:


**for we had said** For we had already said to King Darius that the Holy One, blessed be He, benefits those who seek Him and pours out His wrath and His anger upon those who forsake Him.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:23:1)


**וַיֵּעָתֵר לָנוּ.** וְקִבֵּל תְּפִלָּתֵינוּ שֶׁבָּאנוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם לְשָׁלוֹם:


**and He accepted our prayer** for we arrived safely in Jerusalem.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:24:1)


**וָאַבְדִּילָה.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר הָיִיתִי שָׁם עַל נְהַר אַהֲוָא הִבְדַּלְתִּי מִשָּׂרֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים וְשָׁקַלְתִּי לָהֶם הַכֶּסֶף וְהַזָּהָב לִיתֵּן בְּאוֹצְרוֹת בֵּית ה׳:


**And I separated** And when I was there by the river Ahava, I separated some of the chiefs of the priests and weighed out the silver and the gold for them to deposit into the treasuries of the House of the Lord.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:25:1)


**הַהֵרִימוּ.** אֲשֶׁר הֵרִימוּ הַנִּמְצָאִים בְּבָבֶל:


**which...had offered** which those living in Babylon had offered.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:26:1)


**וּכְלֵי כֶסֶף מֵאָה לַכִּכָּרִים.** כִּכָּר בְּכָל כְּלִי:


**and one hundred silver vessels by talents** one talent for each vessel.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:1)


**וּכְפוֹרֵי.** מִזְרָקִים:


**And...sprinkling bowls** bowls for sprinkling [the blood of the sacrifices].


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:2)


**לַאֲדַרְכֹּנִים אָלֶף.** מִין מַטְבֵּעַ, אֶלֶף דַּרְכּוֹנִים הָיוּ בְּעֶשְׂרִים **כְּפֹרֵי זָהָב** הַלָּלוּ:


**of one thousand darics** a type of coin. There were one thousand darics in these twenty sprinkling bowls of gold.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:3)


**מֻצְהָב.** מִשֶּׁל נְחֹשֶׁת מֻצְהָב, (אוֹ) מִין מַטְבֵּעַ שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת:


**golden** of golden copper (or) a type of copper coin.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:4)


**טוֹבָה.** לְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה קוֹרֵא לַנְּחֹשֶׁת:


**fine** Heb. טוֹבָה, the feminine form, modifying copper (נְחֹשֶּׁת).


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:5)


**שְׁנַיִם.** שְׁנֵי כֵלִים:


**two** two vessels.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:27:6)


**חֲמוּדוֹת כַּזָּהָב.** הַכֵּלִים קוֹרֵא חֲמוּדוֹת בִּלְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה:


**precious as gold** Heb. חֲמוּדוֹת כַּזָהָב. He calls the vessels חֲמוּדוֹת in the feminine form.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:28:1)


**אֲלֵיהֶם.** לְאוֹתָן שָׂרֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים:


**to them** to these chiefs of the priests.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:29:1)


**הַלִּשְׁכוֹת בֵּית ה׳.** כְּדֵי לִיתֵּן בַּלִּשְׁכוֹת הַבַּיִת:


**the chambers of the House of the Lord** in order to deposit them in the chambers of the Temple.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:31:1)


**בִּשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר לַחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן.** בִּשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר לְנִיסָן:


**on the twelfth of the first month** on the twelfth of Nissan


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:31:2)
