**וְיַד אֱלֹהֵינוּ הָיְתָה עָלֵינוּ.** לְהַצְלִיחֵנוּ עַל הַדֶּרֶךְ אֲשֶׁר הָלַכְנוּ עָלֶיהָ:


**the hand of our God was upon us** to cause us to succeed in the way that we traversed.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:34:1)


**בְּמִסְפָּר בְּמִשְׁקָל לַכֹּל.** מִסְפַּר הַכֵּלִים וּמִשְׁקָלָם כַּאֲשֶׁר קִבְּלוּהוּ כָּךְ הָיָה נִשְׁקָל כֻּלּוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר פְּרָעוּהוּ:


**Everything was counted and weighed** The number of the vessels and their weights was the same when they received them as when they itemized them.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:35:1)


**הַבָּאִים מֵהַשְּׁבִי בְּנֵי הַגּוֹלָה.** הַלָּלוּ אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ עִם עֶזְרָא:


**Those coming from captivity, the people of the exile** those who came with Ezra.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:35:2)


**הִקְרִיבוּ עוֹלוֹת.** הַכֹּל עוֹלָה, וְהוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה הָיְתָה:


**offered up burnt offerings** all were burnt offerings, and it was a temporary ruling.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Ezra 8:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:1)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ אֶת דָּתֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** הַלָּלוּ עוֹלֵי גוֹלָה סִפְּרוּ מִצְוַת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ לִגְדוֹלֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ רוֹאֵי פָנָיו:


**And they gave the king’s decrees** These returnees from exile related King Darius’s mandate to the officers of the king, those who see his face.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:2)


**וּפַחֲווֹת.** כְּמוֹ וּפַחוֹת:


**and the governors** Heb. וּפַחֲווֹת, like וְפַחוֹת.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:3)


**עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר.** אוֹתָן שֶׁל צַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵן בְּעֵבֶר הַנָּהָר לְיוֹשְׁבֵי בָּבֶל:


**the land beyond the river** those on the side of the land of Israel, who are considered as being beyond the river of the inhabitants of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezra 8:36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/8:36:4)


**וְנָשְׂאוּ.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְנַשְּׂאִין וּמְסַיְּעִין:


**and they exalted** They were exalting and assisting.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:1:1)


**אֵלֶּה.** דְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ אֲשֶׁר סִפְּרוּ דָּתֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**these** these incidents that related the kings’ decrees.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:1:2)


**וְהַשָּׂרִים.** שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**the chiefs** of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:1:3)


**לֵאמֹר.** וְכֵן אָמְרוּ לִי:


**saying** And so they said to me.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:2:1)


**הָיְתָה בַּמַּעַל הַזֶּה רִאשׁוֹנָה.** שֶׁהֵם הִתְחִילוּ לִמְעוֹל מַעַל לְהִתְחַתֵּן **בְּגוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת**:


**was first in this treachery** for they commenced to deal treacherously by intermarrying with the peoples of the lands.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:3:1)


**מְשׁוֹמֵם.** עָלוּב וְשׁוֹמֵם, כְּמוֹ (יחזקאל ג:טו) ״שִׁבְעַת יָמִים מַשְׁמִים בְּתוֹכָם״:


**bewildered** Heb. מְשּׁוֹמֵם miserable and bewildered, like (Ez. 3:15): “...seven days bewildered among them.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:5:1)


**מִתַּעֲנִיתִי.** שֶׁלֹּא אָכַלְתִּי אוֹתוֹ יוֹם:


**from my fast** for I had not eaten on that day.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:7:1)


**נָתַנּוּ.** כְּמוֹ נְתַנְנוּ, דָּגֵשׁ הַנּוּ״ן תַּחַת חֶסְרוֹן נוּ״ן אַחֶרֶת, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי עָלֶיךָ תִשְּׁעַנוּ״ (דברי הימים ב יד:יד) כְּמוֹ נִשְׁעַנְנוּ, ״וְנָתַנּוּ אֶת בְּנֹתֵינוּ״ (בראשית לד:טז) כְּמוֹ וְנָתַנְנוּ:


**we were delivered** Heb. נִתַּנוּ, like נִתַּנְנוּ. The “dagesh” in the “nun” is instead of another “nun,” like (II Chron. 14:10): “for we rely (תִשְּׁעַנוּ) on You,” like נִשְּׁעַנְנוּ. (Gen. 34:16): “... then we will give (וְנָתַנוּ) our daughters,” like וְנָתַנְנוּ.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:7:2)


**כְּהַיּוֹם הַזֶּה.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּרְאֶה הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה, שֶׁכְּבָר גָּלוּ עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים וַעֲדַיִן נִשְׁאֲרוּ בְּבָבֶל הַרְבֵּה מִן הַגּוֹלִים:


**as of this day** as appears this day, for the Ten Tribes have already been exiled, and many of the exiles still remain in Babylon.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:1)


**וְעַתָּה כִּמְעַט רֶגַע.** לְפִי שָׁעָה:


**And now, for a short moment** temporarily.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:2)


**תְּחִנָּה.** כְּמוֹ חֲנִינָה:


**favor** Heb. תְּחִנָה, like חֲנִינָה


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:3)


**וְלָתֶת לָנוּ יָתֵד.** לִקְבֹּעַ אוֹתָנוּ בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and to give us a peg** to establish us in the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:4)


**יָתֵד.** כְּמוֹ (ישעיהו כב:כג) ״וּתְקַעְתִּיו יָתֵד בְּמָקוֹם נֶאֱמָן״:


**a peg** יָתֵד, like (Isa. 22:23): “And I will thrust him like a peg (יָתֵד) in a sure place.”


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:8:5)


**מְחְיָה מְעַט.** חַיִּים מוּעָטִים:


**a little life** [as translated,] a little life.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:1)


**כִּי עֲבָדִים אֲנַחְנוּ.** לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרָס:


**For we are slaves** to Darius, king of Persia.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:2)


**לֹא עֲזָבָנוּ.** לֹא עָזַב אוֹתָנוּ:


**has not forsaken us** Heb. לֹא עֲזָבָנוּ, a combination of לֹא עָזַב אוֹתָנוּ.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:3)


**וַיֵּט.** לְשׁוֹן מִפְעָל כְּמוֹ ״מַטֶּה״, הַטֵּה עָלֵינוּ חַסְדּוֹ:


**and He has extended** Heb. וַיַט, an expression of the causative, like מַטֶה. He extended His loving-kindness upon us.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:9:4)


**גָּדֵר.** שֶׁהָיוּ גּוֹדְרִין גָּדֵר לִשְׁמֹר מִצְוֹתָיו:


**a fence** for they had created a fence to keep His commandments.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:11:1)


**נִדָּה.** טְמֵאָה וּגְעוּלָה:


**unclean** Heb. נִדָה, unclean and repulsive.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:12:1)


**וְהוֹרַשְׁתֶּם.** אֶת הָאָרֶץ לִבְנֵיכֶם עַד עוֹלָם:


**and you shall cause...to inherit** You shall give the land to your children as an inheritance to eternity.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:13:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵי כָּל הַבָּא עָלֵינוּ.** אַחֲרֵי כָּל הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הָרָע אֲשֶׁר הִגִּיעַ עָלֵינוּ בְּמַעֲשֵׂינוּ הָרָעִים:


**And after all that has come upon us** after all the calamity that has befallen us because of our evil deeds.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:13:2)


**חָשַׂכְתָּ לְמַטָּה מֵעֲוֺנֵנוּ.** מָנַעְתָּ מֵעֲוֺנוֹתֵינוּ עַד **לְמַטָּה** שֶׁנִּפְחַת עֲוֺנֵינוּ עַל כַּפָּרַת גָּלוּתֵנוּ. וְיֵשׁ לָנוּ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בְּעִנְיָן זֶה: מָנַעְתָּ עַצְמְךָ מִלִּגְבּוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ כָּל עֲוֺנֵינוּ, וְגָבִיתָ מִמֶּנּוּ **לְמַטָּה** מִן הָעֲוֺנוֹת, וְלֹא פָרַעְתָּ מִמֶּנּוּ כְּכָל חַטֹּאתֵינוּ:


**You have punished less** You have held back from our iniquities until below them [i.e., less than them], for our iniquity was lessened by the expiation effected by our exile. We may also interpret it in this manner: You held Yourself back from collecting from us all our iniquity, and You collected from us below [less than] the iniquities, and You requited us according to all our sins.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:14:1)


**הֲנָשִׁיב.** אִם נָשׁוּב:


**If we revert** Heb. הֲנָשּׁוּב, if we return.


###### Rashi on Ezra 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:14:2)


**הֲלֹא תֶאֱנַף בָּנוּ.** תְּמִיהָה הַמִּתְקַיֶּמֶת:


**will You not be wroth with us** a wonder with an affirmative answer.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/9:15:1)


**כִּי אֵין.** כֹּחַ מַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים בָּנוּ לַעֲמֹד וּלְהִתְקַיֵּם לְפָנֶיךָ עַל עֲבֵירָה זוֹ:


**for we cannot** lit. for there is nothing; i.e., we do not have the power of good deeds to stand and exist before You, because of this transgression.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:1:1)


**בֶכֶה**. כְּמוֹ בֶּכִי:


**weeping** Heb. בּכֶה, like בֶּכִי.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:2:1)


**וְנוֹשֵׁב.** וְהוֹשַׁבְנוּם:


**and we have taken** And we took them in.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:2:2)


**יֵשׁ מִקְוֶה.** יֵשׁ תִּקְוָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַל כָּךְ שֶׁהֵם יְכוֹלִים לְהוֹצִיא נְשֵׁיהֶם הָעוֹבְדוֹת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת וְהַנּוֹלָד מֵהֶם וּלְהוֹצִיא כָּל הַבָּנִים הַנּוֹלָדִים מִן הַנָּשִׁים הָעוֹבְדוֹת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**there is hope** There is hope for Israel concerning this, for they can cast out their foreign wives and their offspring and cast out all the children born from these foreign wives.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:3:1)


**בַּעֲצַת אֲדֹנָי.** בַּעֲצָתוֹ וּבִרְצוֹנוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**by the counsel of the Lord** by the counsel and the will of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:3:2)


**וַחֲרֵדִים בְּמִצְוַת אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** וְיִרְאֵי שָׁמַיִם יוֹצִיאֵם:


**and those who hasten to [perform] the commandment of our God** and the God-fearers, they shall cast them out.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:3:3)


**וְכַתּוֹרָה יֵעָשֶׂה.** הָעוֹשֶׂה הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר יוֹצִיא אִשְׁתּוֹ וּבָנָיו הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים:


**and according to the Law it shall be done** he who performs this act, who will cast out his gentile wife and children.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:4:1)


**וַאֲנַחְנוּ עַמְּךָ.** לִהְיוֹת מַסְכִּימִים עִמָּךְ:


**and we are with you** to agree with you.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:7:1)


**קוֹל.** כָּרוֹז:


**a proclamation** Heb. קוֹל, lit. a voice, an announcement.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:8:1)


**לִשְׁלֹשֶׁת הַיָּמִים.** לְסוֹף שְׁלֹשֶׁת הַיָּמִים:


**by three days** at the end of three days.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:9:1)


**חֹדֶשׁ הַתְּשִׁיעִי.** הוּא חֹדֶשׁ כִּסְלֵו:


**the ninth month** That is the month of Kislev.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:9:2)


**מְרַעֲדִים עַל הַדָּבָר.** מִתְיָרְאִים עַל הַדָּבָר וּמִתְיָרְאִים הָיוּ מִמִּכְשׁוֹל שֶׁל עֲבֵרָה זֹאת:


**quaking over the matter** They were afraid over the matter. They were afraid of the stumbling block of this transgression.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:9:3)


**וּמֵהַגְּשָׁמִים.** וּמִן הַגְּשָׁמִים הַיּוֹרְדִים עֲלֵיהֶם שָׁם הָיוּ רְעוּדִים וַעֲלוּבִים:


**and because of the rains** And because of the rains that were falling upon them there, they were shivering and miserable.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:11:1)


**תּוֹדָה**. וִדּוּי:


**confess** Heb. תּוֹדָה, [which, in this case, means] וִדּוּי, confession.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:13:1)


**וְהָעֵת גְּשָׁמִים.** שֶׁהַגְּשָׁמִים יוֹרְדִים עָלֵינוּ:


**and it is the rainy season** for the rains are falling upon us.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:13:2)


**וְהַמְּלָאכָה.** וְהַרְחָקַת עֲבֵרָה זוֹ אֵין אַתָּה יָכוֹל לְתַקֵּן כָּל כָּךְ בִּמְהִירוּת:


**and the work** And this separation from sin cannot be executed so quickly.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:14:1)


**לְכָל הַקָּהָל.** עִם כָּל הַקָּהָל:


**with the entire congregation** Heb. לְכָל הַקָהָל


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:14:2)


**הַהוֹשִׁיב.** אֲשֶׁר הוֹשִׁיב:


**who took in** Heb. הַהשִּׁיב equivalent to אֲשֶּׁר הוֹשִּׁיב.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:14:3)


**עַד לְהָשִׁיב.** עַד אֲשֶׁר יָשׁוּב חֲרוֹנוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵעָלֵינוּ:


**until we avert** until the burning anger of the Holy One, blessed be He, returns from upon us.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:15:1)


**עָמְדוּ עַל זֹאת.** לְקַנְתֵּר עַל דָּבָר זֶה:


**stood up against this** to argue concerning this matter.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:15:2)


**עֲזָרוּם.** הֶחֱזִיקוּ יְדֵיהֶם:


**supported them** strengthened their hands.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:16:1)


**לְדַרְיוֹשׁ.** כְּמוֹ לִדְרוֹשׁ:


**to investigate** Heb. לְדַרְיוֹשּׁ, like לִדְרוֹשּׁ.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:17:1)


**וַיְכַלּוּ בַכֹּל.** וְהִשְׁלִימוּ אֶת תִּקּוּן הַמַּעֲשֶׂה מִן הָאֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר הוֹשִׁיבוּ נָשִׁים שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים כִּי הוֹצִיאָם:


**And they completed everything** And they completed the rectification of the matter of the men who had brought in heathen wives, for they cast them out.


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:17:2)


**לַחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן.** עַד יוֹם אֶחָד בְּנִיסָן:


**of the first month** until the first day of Nissan.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:19:1)


**יָדָם.** עֲצָתָם, כְּמוֹ ״הֲיַד יוֹאָב אִתָּךְ בְּכָל זֹאת״ (שמואל ב יד:יט):


**And they advised them** Heb. וַיִתְּנוּ יָדָם, lit. and they gave their hand, their advice, like (II Sam. 14:19): “Behold Joab’s advice (יַד יוֹאָב) is with you in all this (sic).”


###### Rashi on Ezra 10:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:19:2)


**עַל אַשְׁמָתָם.** הוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה הָיְתָה:


**for their guilt** This was a temporary ruling.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:20:1)


**וּמִבְּנֵי אִמֵּר.** כָּל אֵלּוּ כֹּהֲנִים עַד **וּמִן הַלְוִיִּם וְגוֹמֵר**:


**And of the sons of Immer** All these are priests until (verse 23): “And of the Levites, etc.”



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Ezra 10:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezra/r/10:44:1)


**וְיֵשׁ מֵהֶם נָשִׁים וַיָּשִׂימוּ בָנִים.** מִקְצָתָן שֶׁהָיוּ לָהֶם נָשִׁים שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁנּוֹלְדוּ לָהֶם מֵהֶם בָּנִים, וְהַכֹּל הוֹצִיאוּ הַנָּשִׁים וְהַבָּנִים:


**and some of them had wives by whom they had children** And some of them had heathen wives by whom they had children. They cast out all of them, both the wives and the children.
